ARCHIVO: apps/console/README.md

# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: apps/console/eslint.config.js
===========================================================================


import js from '@eslint/js'
import globals from 'globals'
import reactHooks from 'eslint-plugin-react-hooks'
import reactRefresh from 'eslint-plugin-react-refresh'
import tseslint from 'typescript-eslint'
import { defineConfig, globalIgnores } from 'eslint/config'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      js.configs.recommended,
      tseslint.configs.recommended,
      reactHooks.configs.flat.recommended,
      reactRefresh.configs.vite,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      globals: globals.browser,
    },
  },
])

  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="root"></div>
    <script type="module" src="/src/main.tsx"></script>
  </body>
</html>

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

import "./index.css";

import { presenceAdapter } from "./presence/adapter";
import {
  loadLearningCandidates,
  removeLearningCandidate,
  saveLearningCandidate,
  updateLearningCandidate,
} from "./presence/learningCandidates";

import {
  buildCycleHistory,
  getHistoricalCycles,
  getCurrentCycle,
} from "./presence/cycles";

import type {
  NexusPresenceState,
  NexusPresenceStateModel,
} from "./presence/types";

type NavItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
};

type TimelineStatus =
  | "done"
  | "active"
  | "pending"
  | "failed";

type TimelineEvent = {
  id: string;
  time: string;
  label: string;
  status: TimelineStatus;
  state: NexusPresenceState;
  eventType: string;
  data?: unknown;
  detail?: string;
};

type HistoricalReplayEvent = {
  id: string;
  time: string;
  label: string;
  eventType: string;
  data?: unknown;
  detail?: string;
};

type CycleComparisonRow = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  left: string;
  right: string;
  changed: boolean;
};

type CycleInsight = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
  tone: "stable" | "change" | "difference";
  evidenceKeys: string[];
};

type InsightLearningCandidate = {
  id: string;
  insightId: string;
  title: string;
  knowledge: string;
  sourceCycleIds: string[];
  evidenceKeys: string[];
  createdAt: string;
  status?: "staged" | "promoted";
  promotedAt?: string;
  memoryKey?: string;
};

type FocusField = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
};

type FocusSemantic = {
  label: string;
  eventType: string;
};

function getCycleRequestText(
  cycle: { request?: { message?: string; data?: unknown } } | null,
): string {
  const requestData = cycle?.request?.data;

  if (requestData && typeof requestData === "object") {
    const record = requestData as Record<string, unknown>;

    const candidates = [
      record.text,
      record.original_text,
      record.request,
    ];

    const requestValue = candidates.find(
      (value): value is string =>
        typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0,
    );

    if (requestValue) {
      return requestValue;
    }
  }

  if (
    typeof cycle?.request?.message === "string" &&
    cycle.request.message.trim().length > 0
  ) {
    return cycle.request.message;
  }

  return "Cognitive cycle";
}

function getFocusSemantic(eventType: string): FocusSemantic {
  switch (eventType) {
    case "user_request":
    case "cognitive_context":
      return {
        label: "INPUT",
        eventType,
      };

    case "intent":
    case "decision":
      return {
        label: "REASONING",
        eventType,
      };

    case "objective":
    case "plan":
    case "plan_final":
      return {
        label: "OBJECTIVE",
        eventType,
      };

    case "execution_result":
      return {
        label: "EXECUTION",
        eventType,
      };

    case "verification":
    case "verification_failure":
      return {
        label: "EVIDENCE",
        eventType,
      };

    case "episode":
      return {
        label: "MEMORY",
        eventType,
      };

    default:
      return {
        label: "COGNITION",
        eventType,
      };
  }
}

function buildInsightLearningCandidate(
  insight: CycleInsight | null,
  leftCycle: { id: string } | null,
  rightCycle: { id: string } | null,
): InsightLearningCandidate | null {
  if (!insight || !leftCycle || !rightCycle) {
    return null;
  }

  const knowledgeByInsight: Record<string, string> = {
    "same-intent-different-execution":
      "Cuando NEXUS identifica el mismo intento con objetivos de ejecución diferentes, debe conservar la relación entre intención y aplicación como evidencia contextual del ciclo.",
    "same-decision-different-result":
      "Una misma decisión puede producir resultados diferentes; la decisión no debe considerarse suficiente para explicar el resultado sin consultar la evidencia de ejecución.",
    "plan-changed":
      "Cambios en la secuencia de pasos deben conservarse como una diferencia de estrategia entre ciclos comparables.",
    "objective-changed":
      "Un cambio de objetivo indica que ciclos aparentemente similares pueden estar resolviendo metas distintas y deben mantenerse separados en el aprendizaje.",
    "verification-differs":
      "La verificación debe conservarse como evidencia independiente del resultado de ejecución para explicar por qué dos ciclos no son equivalentes.",
    "reasoning-stable":
      "Cuando intención y decisión permanecen estables entre ciclos, esa estabilidad puede utilizarse como referencia al analizar diferencias posteriores en ejecución o verificación.",
  };

  const sourceCycleIds = [leftCycle.id, rightCycle.id].sort();

  return {
    id: `insight-${insight.id}-${sourceCycleIds.join("--")}`,
    insightId: insight.id,
    title: insight.title,
    knowledge:
      knowledgeByInsight[insight.id] ??
      `El patrón “${insight.title}” fue observado entre dos ciclos y queda registrado como candidato para conocimiento reutilizable.`,
    sourceCycleIds,
    evidenceKeys: insight.evidenceKeys,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    status: "staged",
  };
}

const navigation: NavItem[] = [
  { id: "overview", label: "Overview", icon: "⌂" },

  { id: "learning", label: "Learning", icon: "✦" },
  { id: "events", label: "Events", icon: "◷" },
];

  recovering: "Recovering...",
};

function formatTime(timestamp?: string): string {
  if (!timestamp) {
    return "--:--:--";
  }

  const date = new Date(timestamp);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return "--:--:--";
  }

  return date.toLocaleTimeString("es-AR", {
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    second: "2-digit",
  });
}

function eventLabel(type: string, message?: string): string {
  if (message) {
    return message;
  }

  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    request: "Request processed",
    context: "Context recovered",
    intent: "Intent identified",
    decision: "Decision evaluated",
    objective: "Objective defined",
    plan: "Plan generated",
    execution: "Action executed",
    verification: "Execution verified",
    memory: "Episode stored",
    learning: "Experience learned",
  };

  return labels[type] ?? type;
}

function getEventDetail(
  eventType: string,
  data?: unknown,
): string | undefined {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    return undefined;
  }

  const record = data as Record<string, unknown>;

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.13 — COGNITIVE FOCUS
   * ============================================================
   */

  if (eventType === "user_request") {
    const text =
      typeof record.text === "string"
        ? record.text
        : undefined;

    return text;
  }

  if (eventType === "cognitive_context") {
    const keys = Object.keys(record);

    if (keys.length > 0) {
      return `${keys.length} context fields`;
    }

    return undefined;
  }

  if (eventType === "intent") {
    const action =
      typeof record.action === "string"
        ? record.action
        : undefined;

    const confidence =
      typeof record.confidence === "number"
        ? record.confidence
        : undefined;

    if (action && confidence !== undefined) {
      return `${action} · confidence ${confidence.toFixed(2)}`;
    }

    if (action) {
      return action;
    }

    if (confidence !== undefined) {
      return `confidence ${confidence.toFixed(2)}`;
    }

    return undefined;
  }

  if (eventType === "decision") {
    const action =
      typeof record.selected_action === "string"
        ? record.selected_action
        : undefined;

    const confidence =
      typeof record.confidence === "number"
        ? record.confidence
        : undefined;

    const reason =
      typeof record.reason === "string"
        ? record.reason
        : undefined;

    const parts: string[] = [];

    if (action) {
      parts.push(action);
    }

    if (confidence !== undefined) {
      parts.push(`confidence ${confidence.toFixed(2)}`);
    }

    if (reason) {
      parts.push(reason);
    }

    return parts.length > 0
      ? parts.join(" · ")
      : undefined;
  }

  if (eventType === "objective") {
    const title =
      typeof record.title === "string"
        ? record.title
        : undefined;

    const status =
      typeof record.status === "string"
        ? record.status
        : undefined;

    let application: string | undefined;

    if (
      record.metadata &&
      typeof record.metadata === "object"
    ) {
      const metadata =
        record.metadata as Record<string, unknown>;

      if (
        typeof metadata.application === "string"
      ) {
        application = metadata.application;
      }
    }

    const parts: string[] = [];

    if (title) {
      parts.push(title);
    }

    if (status) {
      parts.push(`status ${status}`);
    }

    if (application) {
      parts.push(`application ${application}`);
    }

    return parts.length > 0
      ? parts.join(" · ")
      : undefined;
  }

  if (
    eventType === "plan" ||
    eventType === "plan_final"
  ) {
    const steps = Array.isArray(record.steps)
      ? record.steps
      : [];

    if (steps.length > 0) {
      const firstStep = steps[0];

      if (
        firstStep &&
        typeof firstStep === "object"
      ) {
        const step =
          firstStep as Record<string, unknown>;

        const action =
          typeof step.action === "string"
            ? step.action
            : undefined;

        const status =
          typeof step.status === "string"
            ? step.status
            : undefined;

        const parts: string[] = [];

        if (action) {
          parts.push(action);
        }

        if (status) {
          parts.push(`status ${status}`);
        }

        if (steps.length > 1) {
          parts.push(`${steps.length} steps`);
        }

        if (parts.length > 0) {
          return parts.join(" · ");
        }
      }
    }

    const completed =
      typeof record.completed === "boolean"
        ? record.completed
        : undefined;

    if (completed !== undefined) {
      return completed
        ? "plan completed"
        : "plan pending";
    }

    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    eventType === "verification" ||
    eventType === "verification_failure"
  ) {
    const verified =
      typeof record.verified === "boolean"
        ? record.verified
        : undefined;

    const confidence =
      typeof record.confidence === "number"
        ? record.confidence
        : undefined;

    const reason =
      typeof record.reason === "string"
        ? record.reason
        : undefined;

    const parts: string[] = [];

    if (verified !== undefined) {
      parts.push(
        verified
          ? "verified"
          : "not verified",
      );
    }

    if (confidence !== undefined) {
      parts.push(
        `confidence ${confidence.toFixed(2)}`,
      );
    }

    if (reason) {
      parts.push(reason);
    }

    return parts.length > 0
      ? parts.join(" · ")
      : undefined;
  }

  if (eventType === "execution_result") {
    const success =
      typeof record.success === "boolean"
        ? record.success
        : undefined;

    const message =
      typeof record.message === "string"
        ? record.message
        : undefined;

    let output:
      | Record<string, unknown>
      | undefined;

    if (
      record.output &&
      typeof record.output === "object"
    ) {
      output =
        record.output as Record<string, unknown>;
    }

    const parts: string[] = [];

    if (success !== undefined) {
      parts.push(
        success
          ? "success"
          : "failed",
      );
    }

    if (output) {
      const application =
        typeof output.application === "string"
          ? output.application
          : undefined;

      const status =
        typeof output.status === "string"
          ? output.status
          : undefined;

      if (application) {
        parts.push(application);
      }

      if (status) {
        parts.push(status);
      }
    }

    if (message) {
      parts.push(message);
    }

    return parts.length > 0
      ? parts.join(" · ")
      : undefined;
  }

  if (eventType === "episode") {
    return "Experience stored for future reuse.";
  }

  return undefined;
}

function getFocusFields(
  eventType: string,
  data?: unknown,
): FocusField[] {
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
    return [];
  }

  const record = data as Record<string, unknown>;

  if (eventType === "user_request") {
    const text =
      typeof record.text === "string"
        ? record.text
        : undefined;

    return text
      ? [{ label: "REQUEST", value: text }]
      : [];
  }

  if (eventType === "cognitive_context") {
    return [
      {
        label: "FIELDS",
        value: `${Object.keys(record).length}`,
      },
    ];
  }

  if (eventType === "intent") {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.action === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "ACTION",
        value: record.action,
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.confidence === "number") {
      fields.push({
        label: "CONFIDENCE",
        value: record.confidence.toFixed(2),
      });
    }

    if (
      record.parameters &&
      typeof record.parameters === "object"
    ) {
      fields.push({
        label: "PARAMETERS",
        value: JSON.stringify(record.parameters),
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.original_text === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "ORIGINAL",
        value: record.original_text,
      });
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (eventType === "decision") {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.selected_action === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "ACTION",
        value: record.selected_action,
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.confidence === "number") {
      fields.push({
        label: "CONFIDENCE",
        value: record.confidence.toFixed(2),
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.requires_execution === "boolean") {
      fields.push({
        label: "EXECUTION",
        value: record.requires_execution
          ? "required"
          : "not required",
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.reason === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "REASON",
        value: record.reason,
      });
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (eventType === "objective") {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.id === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "ID",
        value: record.id,
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.title === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "TITLE",
        value: record.title,
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.status === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "STATUS",
        value: record.status,
      });
    }

    if (
      record.metadata &&
      typeof record.metadata === "object"
    ) {
      const metadata =
        record.metadata as Record<string, unknown>;

      if (typeof metadata.application === "string") {
        fields.push({
          label: "APPLICATION",
          value: metadata.application,
        });
      }
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (
    eventType === "plan" ||
    eventType === "plan_final"
  ) {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.objective_id === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "OBJECTIVE",
        value: record.objective_id,
      });
    }

    const steps = Array.isArray(record.steps)
      ? record.steps
      : [];

    fields.push({
      label: "STEPS",
      value: `${steps.length}`,
    });

    if (steps.length > 0) {
      const firstStep = steps[0];

      if (
        firstStep &&
        typeof firstStep === "object"
      ) {
        const step =
          firstStep as Record<string, unknown>;

        if (typeof step.action === "string") {
          fields.push({
            label: "ACTION",
            value: step.action,
          });
        }

        if (typeof step.status === "string") {
          fields.push({
            label: "STATUS",
            value: step.status,
          });
        }

        if (typeof step.description === "string") {
          fields.push({
            label: "DESCRIPTION",
            value: step.description,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    if (typeof record.current_step === "number") {
      fields.push({
        label: "CURRENT STEP",
        value: String(record.current_step),
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.completed === "boolean") {
      fields.push({
        label: "COMPLETED",
        value: record.completed
          ? "yes"
          : "no",
      });
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (
    eventType === "verification" ||
    eventType === "verification_failure"
  ) {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.verified === "boolean") {
      fields.push({
        label: "STATUS",
        value: record.verified
          ? "verified"
          : "not verified",
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.confidence === "number") {
      fields.push({
        label: "CONFIDENCE",
        value: record.confidence.toFixed(2),
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.reason === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "REASON",
        value: record.reason,
      });
    }

    if (Array.isArray(record.evidence)) {
      fields.push({
        label: "EVIDENCE",
        value: `${record.evidence.length} item${
          record.evidence.length === 1
            ? ""
            : "s"
        }`,
      });
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (eventType === "execution_result") {
    const fields: FocusField[] = [];

    if (typeof record.success === "boolean") {
      fields.push({
        label: "STATUS",
        value: record.success
          ? "success"
          : "failed",
      });
    }

    if (typeof record.message === "string") {
      fields.push({
        label: "MESSAGE",
        value: record.message,
      });
    }

    if (
      record.output &&
      typeof record.output === "object"
    ) {
      const output =
        record.output as Record<string, unknown>;

      if (typeof output.application === "string") {
        fields.push({
          label: "APPLICATION",
          value: output.application,
        });
      }

      if (typeof output.status === "string") {
        fields.push({
          label: "RESULT",
          value: output.status,
        });
      }
    }

    return fields;
  }

  if (eventType === "episode") {
    return [
      {
        label: "MEMORY",
        value: "Experience stored for future reuse.",
      },
    ];
  }

  return [];
}

function getTimelineStatus(
  eventIndex: number,
  totalEvents: number,
  state: NexusPresenceState,
): TimelineStatus {
  if (
    state === "failed" &&
    eventIndex === totalEvents - 1
  ) {
    return "failed";
  }

  if (
    (state === "recovering" ||
      state === "needs_confirmation") &&
    eventIndex === totalEvents - 1
  ) {
    return "active";
  }

  if (
    state !== "completed" &&
    state !== "idle" &&
    eventIndex === totalEvents - 1
  ) {
    return "active";
  }

  return "done";
}

function buildTimeline(
  presence: NexusPresenceStateModel,
): TimelineEvent[] {
  const events = presence.events;

  if (events.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.12C — TIMELINE CYCLE ISOLATION
   * ============================================================
   */

  let cycleEvents = events;

  for (let index = events.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    if (events[index].type === "user_request") {
      cycleEvents = events.slice(index);
      break;
    }
  }

  return cycleEvents.map((event, index) => {
    const state = event.type as NexusPresenceState;

    return {
      id: event.id,
      time: formatTime(event.timestamp),
      label: eventLabel(event.type, event.message),
      status: getTimelineStatus(
        index,
        cycleEvents.length,
        presence.state,
      ),
      state,
      eventType: event.type,
      data: event.data,
      detail: getEventDetail(
        event.type,
        event.data,
      ),
    };
  });
}

function getCycleEventData(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
  type: string,
): unknown {
  if (!cycle) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return cycle.events.find((event) => event.type === type)?.data;
}

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  return value && typeof value === "object"
    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
    : null;
}

function textValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim()) {
    return value.trim();
  }

  if (typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean") {
    return String(value);
  }

  return "—";
}

function formatComparisonData(
  type: string,
  data: unknown,
): string {
  const record = asRecord(data);

  if (!record) {
    return "—";
  }

  switch (type) {
    case "user_request":
      return textValue(
        record.text ??
          record.original_text ??
          record.request,
      );

    case "intent":
      return [
        `action=${textValue(record.name ?? record.intent)}`,
        `confidence=${textValue(record.confidence)}`,
      ].join(" · ");

    case "decision":
      return [
        `action=${textValue(record.selected_action ?? record.type)}`,
        `confidence=${textValue(record.confidence)}`,
        record.requires_execution !== undefined
          ? `execute=${textValue(record.requires_execution)}`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ");

    case "objective":
      return [
        textValue(record.title ?? record.description),
        record.status
          ? `status=${textValue(record.status)}`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ");

    case "plan": {
      const steps = Array.isArray(record.steps)
        ? record.steps
            .map((step) => {
              const stepRecord = asRecord(step);
              return textValue(
                stepRecord?.name ??
                  stepRecord?.description ??
                  stepRecord?.id,
              );
            })
            .filter((step) => step !== "—")
        : [];

      return [
        steps.length > 0
          ? `${steps.length} steps`
          : "",
        record.current_step !== undefined
          ? `current=${textValue(record.current_step)}`
          : "",
        record.completed !== undefined
          ? `completed=${textValue(record.completed)}`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ") || "—";
    }

    case "plan_final":
      return [
        record.completed !== undefined
          ? `completed=${textValue(record.completed)}`
          : "",
        Array.isArray(record.steps)
          ? `${record.steps.length} steps`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ") || "—";

    case "execution_result": {
      const output = asRecord(record.output);

      return [
        `success=${textValue(record.success)}`,
        record.message
          ? `message=${textValue(record.message)}`
          : "",
        output?.application
          ? `app=${textValue(output.application)}`
          : "",
        output?.status
          ? `status=${textValue(output.status)}`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ");
    }

    case "verification":
    case "verification_failure":
      return [
        `verified=${textValue(record.verified)}`,
        `confidence=${textValue(record.confidence)}`,
        record.reason
          ? `reason=${textValue(record.reason)}`
          : "",
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" · ");

    case "episode": {
      const lesson =
        record.lesson ??
        record.learning ??
        record.summary;

      return lesson
        ? textValue(lesson)
        : Object.keys(record).length > 0
          ? `${Object.keys(record).length} fields`
          : "—";
    }

    default:
      return Object.keys(record).length > 0
        ? `${Object.keys(record).length} fields`
        : "—";
  }
}

function buildCycleComparisonRows(
  leftCycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
  rightCycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): CycleComparisonRow[] {
  const definitions = [
    ["request", "REQUEST", "user_request"],
    ["intent", "INTENT", "intent"],
    ["decision", "DECISION", "decision"],
    ["objective", "OBJECTIVE", "objective"],
    ["plan", "PLAN", "plan"],
    ["plan_final", "PLAN FINAL", "plan_final"],
    ["execution", "EXECUTION", "execution_result"],
    ["verification", "VERIFICATION", "verification"],
    ["episode", "MEMORY / EPISODE", "episode"],
  ] as const;

  return definitions.map(([key, label, eventType]) => {
    const left = formatComparisonData(
      eventType,
      getCycleEventData(leftCycle, eventType),
    );

    const right = formatComparisonData(
      eventType,
      getCycleEventData(rightCycle, eventType),
    );

    return {
      key,
      label,
      left,
      right,
      changed: left !== right,
    };
  });
}

function getCycleStringField(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
  eventType: string,
  fields: string[],
): string | null {
  const record = asRecord(getCycleEventData(cycle, eventType));

  if (!record) {
    return null;
  }

  for (const field of fields) {
    const value = record[field];

    if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim()) {
      return value.trim();
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function getCycleExecutionApplication(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): string | null {
  const record = asRecord(
    getCycleEventData(cycle, "execution_result"),
  );

  const output = asRecord(record?.output);

  return typeof output?.application === "string"
    ? output.application
    : null;
}

function getCycleExecutionSuccess(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): boolean | null {
  const record = asRecord(
    getCycleEventData(cycle, "execution_result"),
  );

  return typeof record?.success === "boolean"
    ? record.success
    : null;
}

function getCyclePlanSignature(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): string {
  const record = asRecord(
    getCycleEventData(cycle, "plan"),
  );

  const steps = Array.isArray(record?.steps)
    ? record.steps
        .map((step) => {
          const stepRecord = asRecord(step);

          return (
            stepRecord?.action ??
            stepRecord?.name ??
            stepRecord?.description ??
            stepRecord?.id
          );
        })
        .filter(
          (value): value is string =>
            typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0,
        )
        .map((value) => value.trim())
    : [];

  return steps.join(" → ");
}

function getCycleVerificationSignature(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): string {
  const record = asRecord(
    getCycleEventData(cycle, "verification"),
  );

  if (!record) {
    return "";
  }

  return [
    typeof record.verified === "boolean"
      ? String(record.verified)
      : "",
    typeof record.confidence === "number"
      ? record.confidence.toFixed(2)
      : "",
    typeof record.reason === "string"
      ? record.reason.trim()
      : "",
  ].join("|");
}

function buildCycleInsights(
  leftCycle: {
    id: string;
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
  rightCycle: {
    id: string;
    events: Array<{
      type: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): CycleInsight[] {
  if (!leftCycle || !rightCycle || leftCycle.id === rightCycle.id) {
    return [];
  }

  const insights: CycleInsight[] = [];

  const leftIntent = getCycleStringField(
    leftCycle,
    "intent",
    ["name", "intent", "action"],
  );
  const rightIntent = getCycleStringField(
    rightCycle,
    "intent",
    ["name", "intent", "action"],
  );

  const leftDecision = getCycleStringField(
    leftCycle,
    "decision",
    ["selected_action", "type", "action"],
  );
  const rightDecision = getCycleStringField(
    rightCycle,
    "decision",
    ["selected_action", "type", "action"],
  );

  const leftApp = getCycleExecutionApplication(leftCycle);
  const rightApp = getCycleExecutionApplication(rightCycle);

  const leftSuccess = getCycleExecutionSuccess(leftCycle);
  const rightSuccess = getCycleExecutionSuccess(rightCycle);

  const leftPlan = getCyclePlanSignature(leftCycle);
  const rightPlan = getCyclePlanSignature(rightCycle);

  const leftVerification = getCycleVerificationSignature(leftCycle);
  const rightVerification = getCycleVerificationSignature(rightCycle);

  const leftObjective = getCycleStringField(
    leftCycle,
    "objective",
    ["title", "description"],
  );
  const rightObjective = getCycleStringField(
    rightCycle,
    "objective",
    ["title", "description"],
  );

  if (
    leftIntent &&
    rightIntent &&
    leftIntent === rightIntent &&
    leftApp &&
    rightApp &&
    leftApp !== rightApp
  ) {
    insights.push({
      id: "same-intent-different-execution",
      label: "PATTERN",
      title: "Mismo intento · distinta ejecución",
      detail: `Ambos ciclos interpretaron el intento como “${leftIntent}”, pero la ejecución terminó sobre ${leftApp} y ${rightApp}.`,
      tone: "difference",
      evidenceKeys: ["intent", "execution"],
    });
  }

  if (
    leftDecision &&
    rightDecision &&
    leftDecision === rightDecision &&
    leftSuccess !== null &&
    rightSuccess !== null &&
    leftSuccess !== rightSuccess
  ) {
    insights.push({
      id: "same-decision-different-result",
      label: "PATTERN",
      title: "Misma decisión · distinto resultado",
      detail: `La decisión seleccionada fue “${leftDecision}” en ambos ciclos, mientras el resultado de ejecución fue ${leftSuccess ? "exitoso" : "no exitoso"} y ${rightSuccess ? "exitoso" : "no exitoso"}.`,
      tone: "difference",
      evidenceKeys: ["decision", "execution"],
    });
  }

  if (leftPlan && rightPlan && leftPlan !== rightPlan) {
    insights.push({
      id: "plan-changed",
      label: "CHANGE",
      title: "El plan cambió",
      detail: `La secuencia de pasos difiere entre los ciclos: “${leftPlan}” frente a “${rightPlan}”.`,
      tone: "change",
      evidenceKeys: ["plan"],
    });
  }

  if (
    leftObjective &&
    rightObjective &&
    leftObjective !== rightObjective
  ) {
    insights.push({
      id: "objective-changed",
      label: "CHANGE",
      title: "El objetivo cambió",
      detail: `El objetivo registrado fue “${leftObjective}” en un ciclo y “${rightObjective}” en el otro.`,
      tone: "change",
      evidenceKeys: ["objective"],
    });
  }

  if (
    leftVerification &&
    rightVerification &&
    leftVerification !== rightVerification
  ) {
    insights.push({
      id: "verification-changed",
      label: "EVIDENCE",
      title: "La verificación difiere",
      detail: "Los datos de verificación no son iguales entre los dos ciclos comparados.",
      tone: "difference",
      evidenceKeys: ["verification"],
    });
  }

  if (
    leftIntent &&
    rightIntent &&
    leftIntent === rightIntent &&
    leftDecision &&
    rightDecision &&
    leftDecision === rightDecision
  ) {
    insights.push({
      id: "reasoning-stable",
      label: "STABLE",
      title: "Razonamiento estable",
      detail: `El intento “${leftIntent}” condujo a la misma decisión “${leftDecision}” en ambos ciclos.`,
      tone: "stable",
      evidenceKeys: ["intent", "decision"],
    });
  }

  if (insights.length === 0) {
    insights.push({
      id: "no-pattern",
      label: "OBSERVATION",
      title: "No se detectaron patrones definidos",
      detail: "Los datos disponibles no muestran una coincidencia o diferencia suficientemente clara para generar un insight específico.",
      tone: "stable",
      evidenceKeys: [],
    });
  }

  return insights;
}

function buildHistoricalReplayEvents(
  cycle: {
    events: Array<{
      id: string;
      type: string;
      timestamp: string;
      message?: string;
      data?: unknown;
    }>;
  } | null,
): HistoricalReplayEvent[] {
  if (!cycle) {
    return [];
  }

  return cycle.events.map((event) => ({
    id: event.id,
    time: formatTime(event.timestamp),
    label: eventLabel(event.type, event.message),
    eventType: event.type,
    data: event.data,
    detail: getEventDetail(event.type, event.data),
  }));
}

function Core({
  state,
}: {
  state: NexusPresenceState;
}) {
  const isCompleted = state === "completed";
  const isVerifying = state === "verifying";
  const isFailed = state === "failed";

  return (
    <div className={`core-stage state-${state}`}>
      <div className="core-grid" />

      <div className="core-orbit orbit-one" />
      <div className="core-orbit orbit-two" />
      <div className="core-orbit orbit-three" />

      <div className="core-particles">
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
      </div>

      <div className="core">
        <div className="core-inner">
          {isCompleted || isVerifying ? (
            <span className="core-check">✓</span>
          ) : isFailed ? (
            <span className="core-check">!</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="core-point" />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="core-label">
        {stateLabels[state]}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function App() {
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] =
    useState("overview");

  const [presence, setPresence] =
    useState<NexusPresenceStateModel>(
      presenceAdapter.getCurrentState(),
    );

  const [command, setCommand] = useState("");

  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] =
    useState(false);

  const [commandError, setCommandError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [selectedTimelineEventId, setSelectedTimelineEventId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.19 — COGNITIVE CYCLE HISTORY
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [selectedHistoryCycleId, setSelectedHistoryCycleId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.19D — CYCLE COMPARISON
   *
   * Compara dos ciclos históricos sin alterar sus eventos ni
   * ejecutar ninguna acción.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [comparisonLeftCycleId, setComparisonLeftCycleId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [comparisonRightCycleId, setComparisonRightCycleId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.19F — INSIGHT TRACE
   *
   * Selecciona un insight y hace visible la evidencia concreta
   * que lo originó dentro de la comparación.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [selectedInsightId, setSelectedInsightId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.19G — INSIGHT LEARNING CANDIDATE
   *
   * Registra en la sesión un insight como candidato de aprendizaje.
   * No persiste conocimiento ni modifica el razonamiento.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [learningCandidate, setLearningCandidate] =
    useState<InsightLearningCandidate | null>(null);

  const [learningCandidates, setLearningCandidates] =
    useState<InsightLearningCandidate[]>(() => loadLearningCandidates());

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.19C — HISTORICAL CYCLE REPLAY
   *
   * Reproduce únicamente los eventos almacenados del ciclo
   * histórico seleccionado. Nunca ejecuta acciones ni publica
   * nuevos eventos en Presence.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [historyReplayActive, setHistoryReplayActive] =
    useState(false);

  const [historyReplayPlaying, setHistoryReplayPlaying] =
    useState(false);

  const [historyReplayIndex, setHistoryReplayIndex] =
    useState(0);

  const historyReplayTimerRef =
    useRef<number | null>(null);

  function stopHistoryReplayTimer(): void {
    if (historyReplayTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(historyReplayTimerRef.current);
      historyReplayTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  function resetHistoryReplay(): void {
    stopHistoryReplayTimer();
    setHistoryReplayPlaying(false);
    setHistoryReplayActive(false);
    setHistoryReplayIndex(0);
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.17 — COGNITIVE FOCUS AUTO-FOLLOW
   *
   * focusManualSelection:
   *   true  -> usuário assumiu controle manual do Focus.
   *
   * focusAutoFollow:
   *   true  -> o Focus pode acompanhar um ciclo novo.
   *
   * autoFollowTimerRef:
   *   controla uma única reprodução do ciclo.
   *
   * Não usamos setInterval.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [focusManualSelection, setFocusManualSelection] =
    useState(false);

  const [focusAutoFollow, setFocusAutoFollow] =
    useState(true);

  const autoFollowTimerRef =
    useRef<number | null>(null);

  const autoFollowCycleRef =
    useRef<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.18 — COGNITIVE FOCUS REPLAY
   *
   * Replay recorre el último ciclo ya aislado por Timeline.
   * No genera eventos ni ejecuta nuevamente ninguna acción.
   * ============================================================
   */

  const [replayActive, setReplayActive] =
    useState(false);

  const [replayPlaying, setReplayPlaying] =
    useState(false);

  const [replayIndex, setReplayIndex] =
    useState(0);

  const replayTimerRef =
    useRef<number | null>(null);

  function stopReplayTimer(): void {
    if (replayTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(replayTimerRef.current);
      replayTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  function resetReplay(): void {
    stopReplayTimer();
    setReplayPlaying(false);
    setReplayActive(false);
    setReplayIndex(0);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    return presenceAdapter.subscribe(setPresence);
  }, []);

  const timeline = buildTimeline(presence);

  const cycleHistory = buildCycleHistory(presence);
  const historicalCycles = getHistoricalCycles(presence);
  const currentCycle = getCurrentCycle(presence);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (historicalCycles.length < 2) {
      setComparisonLeftCycleId(null);
      setComparisonRightCycleId(null);
      return;
    }

    setComparisonLeftCycleId((current) =>
      current && historicalCycles.some((cycle) => cycle.id === current)
        ? current
        : historicalCycles[1]?.id ?? null,
    );

    setComparisonRightCycleId((current) =>
      current && historicalCycles.some((cycle) => cycle.id === current)
        ? current
        : historicalCycles[0]?.id ?? null,
    );
  }, [historicalCycles.map((cycle) => cycle.id).join("|")]);

  const comparisonLeftCycle =
    historicalCycles.find(
      (cycle) => cycle.id === comparisonLeftCycleId,
    ) ?? null;

  const comparisonRightCycle =
    historicalCycles.find(
      (cycle) => cycle.id === comparisonRightCycleId,
    ) ?? null;

  const cycleComparisonRows =
    buildCycleComparisonRows(
      comparisonLeftCycle,
      comparisonRightCycle,
    );

  const comparisonChangedCount =
    cycleComparisonRows.filter((row) => row.changed).length;

  const cycleInsights = buildCycleInsights(
    comparisonLeftCycle,
    comparisonRightCycle,
  );

  const selectedInsight =
    cycleInsights.find(
      (insight) => insight.id === selectedInsightId,
    ) ?? null;

  function registerLearningCandidate(): void {
    const candidate = buildInsightLearningCandidate(
      selectedInsight,
      comparisonLeftCycle,
      comparisonRightCycle,
    );

    if (!candidate) {
      return;
    }

    saveLearningCandidate(candidate);
    setLearningCandidate(candidate);
    setLearningCandidates(loadLearningCandidates());
  }

  function clearLearningCandidate(): void {
    if (learningCandidate) {
      removeLearningCandidate(learningCandidate);
    }

    setLearningCandidate(null);
    setLearningCandidates(loadLearningCandidates());
  }

  async function promoteLearningCandidate(): Promise<void> {
    if (!learningCandidate || learningCandidate.status === "promoted") {
      return;
    }

    try {
      const response = await fetch("http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/learning/candidates/promote", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(learningCandidate),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Learning promotion failed: ${response.status}`);
      }

      const payload = (await response.json()) as {
        success?: boolean;
        memory_key?: string;
        promoted_at?: string;
      };

      const promoted = {
        ...learningCandidate,
        status: "promoted" as const,
        promotedAt: payload.promoted_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
        memoryKey: payload.memory_key,
      };

      updateLearningCandidate(promoted);
      setLearningCandidate(promoted);
      setLearningCandidates(loadLearningCandidates());
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("NEXUS learning candidate promotion failed", error);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      selectedInsightId &&
      !cycleInsights.some(
        (insight) => insight.id === selectedInsightId,
      )
    ) {
      setSelectedInsightId(null);
    }
  }, [
    selectedInsightId,
    cycleInsights.map((insight) => insight.id).join("|"),
  ]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedInsight || !comparisonLeftCycle || !comparisonRightCycle) {
      setLearningCandidate(null);
      return;
    }

    const stored = learningCandidates.find(
      (candidate) =>
        candidate.insightId === selectedInsight.id &&
        candidate.sourceCycleIds.includes(comparisonLeftCycle.id) &&
        candidate.sourceCycleIds.includes(comparisonRightCycle.id),
    );

    setLearningCandidate(stored ?? null);
  }, [
    selectedInsight?.id,
    comparisonLeftCycle?.id,
    comparisonRightCycle?.id,
    learningCandidates,
  ]);

  const selectedHistoryCycle =
    historicalCycles.find(
      (cycle) => cycle.id === selectedHistoryCycleId,
    ) ?? null;

  const historicalReplayEvents =
    buildHistoricalReplayEvents(selectedHistoryCycle);

  const selectedHistoricalReplayEvent =
    historyReplayActive
      ? historicalReplayEvents[historyReplayIndex] ?? null
      : null;

  const selectedTimelineEventIndex = timeline.findIndex(
    (event) => event.id === selectedTimelineEventId,
  );

  const canNavigateFocusPrevious =
    selectedTimelineEventIndex > 0;

  const canNavigateFocusNext =
    selectedTimelineEventIndex >= 0 &&
    selectedTimelineEventIndex < timeline.length - 1;

  function stopAutoFollow(): void {
    if (autoFollowTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(autoFollowTimerRef.current);
      autoFollowTimerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  function selectPreviousTimelineEvent(): void {
    if (!canNavigateFocusPrevious) {
      return;
    }

    stopAutoFollow();
    resetReplay();

    setFocusManualSelection(true);
    setFocusAutoFollow(false);

    setSelectedTimelineEventId(
      timeline[selectedTimelineEventIndex - 1].id,
    );
  }

  function selectNextTimelineEvent(): void {
    if (!canNavigateFocusNext) {
      return;
    }

    stopAutoFollow();
    resetReplay();

    setFocusManualSelection(true);
    setFocusAutoFollow(false);

    setSelectedTimelineEventId(
      timeline[selectedTimelineEventIndex + 1].id,
    );
  }

  const selectedTimelineEvent =
    replayActive
      ? timeline[replayIndex] ?? null
      : timeline.find(
          (event) => event.id === selectedTimelineEventId,
        ) ?? null;

  function startReplay(): void {
    if (timeline.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    stopAutoFollow();
    stopReplayTimer();

    autoFollowCycleRef.current = timeline[0].id;

    setFocusManualSelection(true);
    setFocusAutoFollow(false);
    setReplayActive(true);
    setReplayPlaying(true);
    setReplayIndex(0);
    setSelectedTimelineEventId(timeline[0].id);
  }

  function toggleReplayPlayback(): void {
    if (!replayActive || timeline.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    setReplayPlaying((playing) => !playing);
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.18A — REPLAY ENGINE
   *
   * Reproduce un ciclo ya existente en la Timeline.
   * ============================================================
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!replayActive || !replayPlaying) {
      stopReplayTimer();
      return;
    }

    if (timeline.length === 0) {
      stopReplayTimer();
      setReplayPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    if (replayIndex >= timeline.length - 1) {
      stopReplayTimer();
      setReplayPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    replayTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const nextIndex = replayIndex + 1;

      setReplayIndex(nextIndex);
      setSelectedTimelineEventId(
        timeline[nextIndex].id,
      );
    }, 650);

    return () => {
      stopReplayTimer();
    };
  }, [
    replayActive,
    replayPlaying,
    replayIndex,
    timeline.length,
    timeline[replayIndex]?.id,
  ]);

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.17B — AUTO-FOLLOW REAL
   *
   * El ciclo se reproduce una sola vez cuando aparece un nuevo
   * ciclo cognitivo.
   *
   * La Timeline puede llegar completa en un snapshot; eso no
   * significa que el Focus deba saltar directamente al último
   * evento.
   *
   * El Focus avanza:
   *
   * 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> ... -> N
   *
   * usando timeouts encadenados.
   *
   * Una interacción manual cancela inmediatamente el proceso.
   * ============================================================
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!focusAutoFollow || focusManualSelection) {
      return;
    }

    if (timeline.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    const cycleId = timeline[0].id;

    if (autoFollowCycleRef.current === cycleId) {
      return;
    }

    autoFollowCycleRef.current = cycleId;

    stopAutoFollow();

    let currentIndex = 0;
    let cancelled = false;

    setSelectedTimelineEventId(timeline[0].id);

    function advance(): void {
      if (cancelled) {
        return;
      }

      if (!focusAutoFollow || focusManualSelection) {
        return;
      }

      currentIndex += 1;

      if (currentIndex >= timeline.length) {
        autoFollowTimerRef.current = null;
        return;
      }

      setSelectedTimelineEventId(
        timeline[currentIndex].id,
      );

      autoFollowTimerRef.current =
        window.setTimeout(advance, 450);
    }

    autoFollowTimerRef.current =
      window.setTimeout(advance, 450);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      stopAutoFollow();
    };
  }, [
    timeline.length,
    timeline[0]?.id,
    focusAutoFollow,
    focusManualSelection,
  ]);

  function startHistoryReplay(): void {
    if (!selectedHistoryCycle || historicalReplayEvents.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    stopHistoryReplayTimer();

    setHistoryReplayActive(true);
    setHistoryReplayPlaying(true);
    setHistoryReplayIndex(0);
  }

  function toggleHistoryReplayPlayback(): void {
    if (!historyReplayActive || historicalReplayEvents.length === 0) {
      return;
    }

    setHistoryReplayPlaying((playing) => !playing);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!historyReplayActive || !historyReplayPlaying) {
      stopHistoryReplayTimer();
      return;
    }

    if (historicalReplayEvents.length === 0) {
      stopHistoryReplayTimer();
      setHistoryReplayPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    if (historyReplayIndex >= historicalReplayEvents.length - 1) {
      stopHistoryReplayTimer();
      setHistoryReplayPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    historyReplayTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setHistoryReplayIndex((index) => index + 1);
    }, 650);

    return () => {
      stopHistoryReplayTimer();
    };
  }, [
    historyReplayActive,
    historyReplayPlaying,
    historyReplayIndex,
    historicalReplayEvents.length,
    selectedHistoryCycleId,
  ]);

  const online = true;

  const requestText =
    presence.request?.text ?? "No active request";

  const intentName =
    presence.intent?.name ?? "—";

  const intentConfidence =
    presence.intent
      ? presence.intent.confidence.toFixed(2)
      : "—";

  const decisionType =
    presence.decision?.type ?? "—";

  const objectiveDescription =
    presence.objective?.description ?? "—";

  const primaryPlanAction =
    presence.plan?.steps?.[0]?.action ?? "—";

  const executionStatus =
    presence.execution?.status ?? "pending";

  const verificationStatus =
    presence.verification?.status ?? "pending";

  const verificationMessage =
    presence.verification?.message ??
    "Verification pending.";

  const isCompleted =
    presence.state === "completed";

  const resultTitle =
    presence.state === "failed"
      ? "FAILED"
      : isCompleted
        ? "COMPLETED"
        : presence.state.toUpperCase();

  async function executeCommand(
    event?: React.FormEvent,
  ): Promise<void> {
    event?.preventDefault();

    const text = command.trim();

    if (!text || isSubmitting) {
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setCommandError(null);

    /*
     * Una nueva solicitud significa un nuevo ciclo.
     * Permitimos que Auto-Follow vuelva a tomar control.
     */

    stopAutoFollow();
    resetReplay();
    resetHistoryReplay();
    autoFollowCycleRef.current = null;
    setFocusManualSelection(false);
    setFocusAutoFollow(true);
    setSelectedTimelineEventId(null);
    setSelectedHistoryCycleId(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        "http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/execute",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            Accept: "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            text,
          }),
        },
      );

      let payload: unknown = null;

      try {
        payload = await response.json();
      } catch {
        payload = null;
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message =
          typeof payload === "object" &&
          payload !== null &&
          "error" in payload &&
          typeof payload.error === "string"
            ? payload.error
            : `NEXUS returned HTTP ${response.status}`;

        throw new Error(message);
      }

      setCommand("");

      await new Promise((resolve) =>
        setTimeout(resolve, 100),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      const message =
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unable to execute request.";

      setCommandError(message);
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  /*
   * ============================================================
   * 9.16 — KEYBOARD NAVIGATION
   * ============================================================
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleTimelineKeyboard(
      event: KeyboardEvent,
    ): void {
      if (!selectedTimelineEventId) {
        return;
      }

      const target =
        event.target as HTMLElement | null;

      if (
        target?.tagName === "INPUT" ||
        target?.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
        target?.isContentEditable
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
        event.preventDefault();
        selectPreviousTimelineEvent();
        return;
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
        event.preventDefault();
        selectNextTimelineEvent();
        return;
      }

      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();

        stopAutoFollow();
        resetReplay();

        setSelectedTimelineEventId(null);
        setFocusManualSelection(true);
        setFocusAutoFollow(false);
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener(
      "keydown",
      handleTimelineKeyboard,
    );

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(
        "keydown",
        handleTimelineKeyboard,
      );
    };
  }, [
    selectedTimelineEventId,
    canNavigateFocusPrevious,
    canNavigateFocusNext,
  ]);

  return (
    <main className="app-shell">
      <div className="ambient ambient-one" />
      <div className="ambient ambient-two" />

      <header className="topbar">
        <div className="brand">
          <span className="brand-name">NEXUS</span>

          <span className="brand-subtitle">
            COGNITIVE RUNTIME
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="system-status">
          <span
            className={`status-dot ${
              online ? "online" : ""
            }`}
          />

          <span>
            {online ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE"}
          </span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="workspace">
        <aside className="sidebar">
          <div className="sidebar-title">
            COGNITIVE SYSTEM
          </div>

          <nav>
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                className={`nav-item ${
                  activeSection === item.id
                    ? "active"
                    : ""
                }`}
                onClick={() =>
                  setActiveSection(item.id)
                }
              >
                <span className="nav-icon">
                  {item.icon}
                </span>

                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <div className="sidebar-footer">
            <div className="runtime-version">
              NEXUS V1
            </div>

            <div className="runtime-mode">
              LOCAL RUNTIME
            </div>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <section className="main-area">
          <div className="page-heading">
            <div>
              <div className="eyebrow">
                SYSTEM OVERVIEW
              </div>

              <h1>Presence</h1>
            </div>

            <div className="session">
              <span className="session-label">
                SESSION
              </span>

              <span className="session-value">
                LOCAL-01
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="dashboard-grid">
            <section className="panel core-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>CORE STATE</span>

                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  LIVE
                </span>
              </div>

              <Core state={presence.state} />

              <div className="core-state-detail">
                <span className="detail-label">
                  CURRENT STATE
                </span>

                <strong>
                  {presence.state.toUpperCase()}
                </strong>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="panel request-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>CURRENT REQUEST</span>

                <span className="request-time">
                  {formatTime(
                    presence.request?.timestamp,
                  )}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="request-text">
                "{requestText}"
              </div>

              <div className="data-stack">
                <div className="data-row">
                  <span>INTENT</span>

                  <strong>{intentName}</strong>

                  <small>
                    {intentConfidence}
                  </small>
                </div>

                <div className="data-row">
                  <span>DECISION</span>

                  <strong>{decisionType}</strong>
                </div>

                <div className="data-row">
                  <span>OBJECTIVE</span>

                  <strong>
                    {objectiveDescription}
                  </strong>
                </div>

                <div className="data-row">
                  <span>PLAN</span>

                  <strong>
                    {primaryPlanAction}
                  </strong>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="panel timeline-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>COGNITIVE TIMELINE</span>

                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  REAL TIME
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="timeline">
                {timeline.map((event) => {
                  const isSelected =
                    selectedTimelineEventId === event.id;

                  return (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={`timeline-item timeline-${event.status}${
                        isSelected
                          ? " timeline-selected"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                      key={event.id}
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (
                          selectedTimelineEventId ===
                          event.id
                        ) {
                          stopAutoFollow();
                          resetReplay();

                          setSelectedTimelineEventId(
                            null,
                          );

                          setFocusManualSelection(
                            true,
                          );

                          setFocusAutoFollow(false);

                          return;
                        }

                        stopAutoFollow();
                        resetReplay();

                        setSelectedTimelineEventId(
                          event.id,
                        );

                        setFocusManualSelection(
                          true,
                        );

                        setFocusAutoFollow(false);
                      }}
                      aria-pressed={isSelected}
                    >
                      <span
                        className={`timeline-node ${event.status}`}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        {event.status === "done"
                          ? "✓"
                          : event.status ===
                              "failed"
                            ? "!"
                            : ""}
                      </span>

                      <span className="timeline-content">
                        <span className="timeline-main">
                          <span className="timeline-time">
                            {event.time}
                          </span>

                          <span className="timeline-label">
                            {event.label}
                          </span>
                        </span>

                        {event.detail && (
                          <span className="timeline-detail">
                            {event.detail}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {selectedTimelineEvent && (
                <div
                  className="timeline-focus"
                  aria-live="polite"
                >
                  <div className="timeline-focus-header">
                    <span>FOCUS</span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="timeline-focus-close"
                      onClick={() => {
                        stopAutoFollow();
                        resetReplay();

                        setSelectedTimelineEventId(
                          null,
                        );

                        setFocusManualSelection(
                          true,
                        );

                        setFocusAutoFollow(false);
                      }}
                      aria-label="Close timeline focus"
                    >
                      ×
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    className="timeline-focus-semantic"
                    data-semantic={
                      getFocusSemantic(
                        selectedTimelineEvent.eventType,
                      ).label
                    }
                  >
                    {
                      getFocusSemantic(
                        selectedTimelineEvent.eventType,
                      ).label
                    }
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-state">
                    {selectedTimelineEvent.state.toUpperCase()}
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-label">
                    {selectedTimelineEvent.label}
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-fields">
                    {getFocusFields(
                      selectedTimelineEvent.eventType,
                      selectedTimelineEvent.data,
                    ).map((field) => (
                      <div
                        key={`${field.label}-${field.value}`}
                        className="timeline-focus-field"
                      >
                        <span>{field.label}</span>

                        <strong>
                          {field.value}
                        </strong>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-time">
                    {selectedTimelineEvent.time}
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-replay">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="timeline-focus-replay-button"
                      onClick={
                        replayPlaying
                          ? toggleReplayPlayback
                          : replayActive
                            ? toggleReplayPlayback
                            : startReplay
                      }
                      disabled={timeline.length === 0}
                      aria-label={
                        replayPlaying
                          ? "Pause cognitive replay"
                          : replayActive
                            ? "Resume cognitive replay"
                            : "Replay cognitive cycle"
                      }
                    >
                      {replayPlaying
                        ? "Ⅱ PAUSE"
                        : replayActive
                          ? "▶ RESUME"
                          : "▶ REPLAY"}
                    </button>

                    {replayActive && (
                      <span className="timeline-focus-replay-status">
                        REPLAY {replayIndex + 1} / {timeline.length}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="timeline-focus-navigation">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="timeline-focus-nav-button"
                      onClick={
                        selectPreviousTimelineEvent
                      }
                      disabled={
                        !canNavigateFocusPrevious
                      }
                      aria-label="Previous cognitive event"
                    >
                      ← PREV
                    </button>

                    <span className="timeline-focus-nav-position">
                      {selectedTimelineEventIndex +
                        1}{" "}
                      / {timeline.length}
                    </span>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="timeline-focus-nav-button"
                      onClick={
                        selectNextTimelineEvent
                      }
                      disabled={
                        !canNavigateFocusNext
                      }
                      aria-label="Next cognitive event"
                    >
                      NEXT →
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            <section className="panel cycle-history-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>COGNITIVE CYCLES</span>

                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  {cycleHistory.length} CYCLES
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="cycle-history-layout">
                <div className="cycle-history-list">
                  <div className="cycle-history-current">
                    <span className="cycle-history-marker">●</span>

                    <div className="cycle-history-summary">
                      <span className="cycle-history-label">CURRENT</span>
                      <strong>
                        {getCycleRequestText(currentCycle)}
                      </strong>
                      <small>
                        {currentCycle?.eventCount ?? timeline.length} EVENTS · {
                          currentCycle?.status.toUpperCase() ??
                          presence.state.toUpperCase()
                        } · {formatTime(currentCycle?.startedAt)}
                      </small>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {historicalCycles.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="cycle-history-empty">
                      No previous cognitive cycles.
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    historicalCycles
                      .slice()
                      .reverse()
                      .map((cycle) => {
                        const isSelected =
                          selectedHistoryCycleId === cycle.id;

                        return (
                          <button
                            key={cycle.id}
                            type="button"
                            className={`cycle-history-item${
                              isSelected
                                ? " cycle-history-selected"
                                : ""
                            }`}
                            onClick={() => {
                              resetHistoryReplay();
                              setSelectedHistoryCycleId(
                                isSelected ? null : cycle.id,
                              );
                            }}
                            aria-pressed={isSelected}
                          >
                            <span className="cycle-history-marker">○</span>

                            <span className="cycle-history-summary">
                              <span className="cycle-history-label">
                                PREVIOUS
                              </span>
                              <strong>{getCycleRequestText(cycle)}</strong>
                              <small>
                                {cycle.eventCount} EVENTS · {
                                  cycle.status.toUpperCase()
                                } · {formatTime(cycle.startedAt)}
                              </small>
                            </span>
                          </button>
                        );
                      })
                  )}
                </div>

                {selectedHistoryCycle && (
                  <div className="cycle-history-detail" aria-live="polite">
                    <div className="cycle-history-detail-header">
                      <span>HISTORY FOCUS</span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="cycle-history-detail-close"
                        onClick={() => {
                          resetHistoryReplay();
                          setSelectedHistoryCycleId(null);
                        }}
                        aria-label="Close history focus"
                      >
                        ×
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="cycle-history-detail-status">
                      {selectedHistoryCycle.status.toUpperCase()}
                    </div>

                    <div className="cycle-history-detail-request">
                      {getCycleRequestText(selectedHistoryCycle)}
                    </div>

                    <div className="cycle-history-detail-fields">
                      <div>
                        <span>EVENTS</span>
                        <strong>
                          {selectedHistoryCycle.eventCount}
                        </strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>STARTED</span>
                        <strong>
                          {formatTime(
                            selectedHistoryCycle.startedAt,
                          )}
                        </strong>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span>ENDED</span>
                        <strong>
                          {formatTime(
                            selectedHistoryCycle.endedAt,
                          )}
                        </strong>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="cycle-history-replay-controls">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="timeline-focus-replay-button"
                        onClick={
                          historyReplayPlaying
                            ? toggleHistoryReplayPlayback
                            : historyReplayActive
                              ? toggleHistoryReplayPlayback
                              : startHistoryReplay
                        }
                        disabled={historicalReplayEvents.length === 0}
                        aria-label={
                          historyReplayPlaying
                            ? "Pause historical cognitive replay"
                            : historyReplayActive
                              ? "Resume historical cognitive replay"
                              : "Replay historical cognitive cycle"
                        }
                      >
                        {historyReplayPlaying
                          ? "Ⅱ PAUSE"
                          : historyReplayActive
                            ? "▶ RESUME"
                            : "▶ REPLAY HISTORY"}
                      </button>

                      {historyReplayActive && (
                        <span className="timeline-focus-replay-status">
                          REPLAY {historyReplayIndex + 1} / {historicalReplayEvents.length}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent && (
                      <div
                        className="cycle-history-replay-focus"
                        aria-live="polite"
                      >
                        <div className="timeline-focus-header">
                          <span>HISTORY FOCUS</span>
                        </div>

                        <div
                          className="timeline-focus-semantic"
                          data-semantic={
                            getFocusSemantic(
                              selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.eventType,
                            ).label
                          }
                        >
                          {getFocusSemantic(
                            selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.eventType,
                          ).label}
                        </div>

                        <div className="timeline-focus-state">
                          {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.eventType.toUpperCase()}
                        </div>

                        <div className="timeline-focus-label">
                          {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.label}
                        </div>

                        <div className="timeline-focus-fields">
                          {getFocusFields(
                            selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.eventType,
