                            selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.data,
                          ).map((field) => (
                            <div
                              key={`${field.label}-${field.value}`}
                              className="timeline-focus-field"
                            >
                              <span>{field.label}</span>
                              <strong>{field.value}</strong>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>

                        {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.detail && (
                          <div className="timeline-focus-time">
                            {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.detail}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="timeline-focus-time">
                          {selectedHistoricalReplayEvent.time}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="cycle-history-detail-events">
                      {selectedHistoryCycle.events.map(
                        (event, index) => {
                          const replaySelected =
                            historyReplayActive &&
                            historyReplayIndex === index;

                          return (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              key={event.id}
                              className={`cycle-history-event${
                                replaySelected
                                  ? " cycle-history-event-selected"
                                  : ""
                              }`}
                              onClick={() => {
                                stopHistoryReplayTimer();
                                setHistoryReplayActive(true);
                                setHistoryReplayPlaying(false);
                                setHistoryReplayIndex(index);
                              }}
                              aria-pressed={replaySelected}
                            >
                              <span>
                                {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                              </span>
                              <strong>
                                {eventLabel(
                                  event.type,
                                  event.message,
                                )}
                              </strong>
                              <small>
                                {formatTime(event.timestamp)}
                              </small>
                            </button>
                          );
                        },
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="panel cycle-comparison-panel">
              {/*
               * 9.19F — INSIGHT TRACE
               * Los insights pueden señalar directamente las
               * dimensiones que los sustentan.
               */}
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>COGNITIVE CYCLE COMPARISON</span>
                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  {historicalCycles.length >= 2
                    ? `${comparisonChangedCount} CHANGED`
                    : "NEEDS 2 CYCLES"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {historicalCycles.length < 2 ? (
                <div className="cycle-comparison-empty">
                  At least two historical cognitive cycles are required
                  for comparison.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="cycle-comparison-content">
                  <div className="cycle-comparison-selectors">
                    <label className="cycle-comparison-selector">
                      <span>CYCLE A</span>
                      <select
                        value={comparisonLeftCycleId ?? ""}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          setComparisonLeftCycleId(
                            event.target.value || null,
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        {historicalCycles
                          .slice()
                          .reverse()
                          .map((cycle) => (
                            <option
                              key={cycle.id}
                              value={cycle.id}
                            >
                              {getCycleRequestText(cycle)} ·{" "}
                              {formatTime(cycle.startedAt)}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                      </select>
                    </label>

                    <div className="cycle-comparison-vs">
                      VS
                    </div>

                    <label className="cycle-comparison-selector">
                      <span>CYCLE B</span>
                      <select
                        value={comparisonRightCycleId ?? ""}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          setComparisonRightCycleId(
                            event.target.value || null,
                          )
                        }
                      >
                        {historicalCycles
                          .slice()
                          .reverse()
                          .map((cycle) => (
                            <option
                              key={cycle.id}
                              value={cycle.id}
                            >
                              {getCycleRequestText(cycle)} ·{" "}
                              {formatTime(cycle.startedAt)}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                      </select>
                    </label>
                  </div>

                  {comparisonLeftCycle &&
                    comparisonRightCycle &&
                    comparisonLeftCycle.id ===
                      comparisonRightCycle.id ? (
                    <div className="cycle-comparison-empty">
                      Select two different cycles to compare them.
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="cycle-comparison-summary">
                        <div>
                          <span>CYCLE A</span>
                          <strong>
                            {comparisonLeftCycle
                              ? getCycleRequestText(
                                  comparisonLeftCycle,
                                )
                              : "—"}
                          </strong>
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <span>CYCLE B</span>
                          <strong>
                            {comparisonRightCycle
                              ? getCycleRequestText(
                                  comparisonRightCycle,
                                )
                              : "—"}
                          </strong>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="cycle-comparison-table">
                        <div className="cycle-comparison-table-head">
                          <span>DIMENSION</span>
                          <span>CYCLE A</span>
                          <span>CYCLE B</span>
                        </div>

                        {cycleComparisonRows.map((row) => (
                          <div
                            key={row.key}
                            className={`cycle-comparison-row${
                              row.changed
                                ? " cycle-comparison-changed"
                                : ""
                            }${
                              selectedInsight?.evidenceKeys.includes(
                                row.key,
                              )
                                ? " cycle-comparison-insight-highlight"
                                : ""
                            }`}
                          >
                            <span className="cycle-comparison-label">
                              {row.label}
                            </span>

                            <span>{row.left}</span>
                            <span>{row.right}</span>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            <section className="panel cycle-insights-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>COGNITIVE CYCLE INSIGHTS</span>
                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  {cycleInsights.length > 0
                    ? `${cycleInsights.length} INSIGHTS`
                    : "NO DATA"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {historicalCycles.length < 2 ||
              !comparisonLeftCycle ||
              !comparisonRightCycle ||
              comparisonLeftCycle.id === comparisonRightCycle.id ? (
                <div className="cycle-insights-empty">
                  Select two different cycles to generate descriptive insights.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="cycle-insights-content">
                  <div className="cycle-insights-context">
                    <span>COMPARING</span>
                    <strong>
                      {getCycleRequestText(comparisonLeftCycle)}
                      <span> ↔ </span>
                      {getCycleRequestText(comparisonRightCycle)}
                    </strong>
                  </div>

                  <div className="cycle-insights-list">
                    {cycleInsights.map((insight) => {
                      const isSelected =
                        selectedInsightId === insight.id;

                      return (
                        <button
                          key={insight.id}
                          type="button"
                          className={`cycle-insight-card cycle-insight-${insight.tone}${
                            isSelected
                              ? " cycle-insight-selected"
                              : ""
                          }`}
                          aria-pressed={isSelected}
                          onClick={() =>
                            setSelectedInsightId(
                              isSelected ? null : insight.id,
                            )
                          }
                        >
                          <div className="cycle-insight-meta">
                            <span>{insight.label}</span>
                            <span>
                              {insight.evidenceKeys.length > 0
                                ? `${insight.evidenceKeys.length} EVIDENCE`
                                : "OBSERVATION"}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <strong>{insight.title}</strong>
                          <p>{insight.detail}</p>
                          <span className="cycle-insight-action">
                            {isSelected
                              ? "HIDE EVIDENCE"
                              : "SHOW EVIDENCE"}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  {selectedInsight && (
                    <div className="cycle-insight-trace">
                      <div className="cycle-insight-trace-header">
                        <span>INSIGHT TRACE</span>
                        <span>
                          {selectedInsight.evidenceKeys.length > 0
                            ? `${selectedInsight.evidenceKeys.length} DIMENSIONS`
                            : "NO DIRECT EVIDENCE"}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <strong>{selectedInsight.title}</strong>

                      {selectedInsight.evidenceKeys.length > 0 ? (
                        <div className="cycle-insight-evidence-list">
                          {selectedInsight.evidenceKeys.map(
                            (key) => {
                              const row =
                                cycleComparisonRows.find(
                                  (item) => item.key === key,
                                );

                              if (!row) {
                                return null;
                              }

                              return (
                                <div
                                  key={key}
                                  className="cycle-insight-evidence-row"
                                >
                                  <span>{row.label}</span>
                                  <span>{row.left}</span>
                                  <span>→</span>
                                  <span>{row.right}</span>
                                </div>
                              );
                            },
                          )}
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <p>
                          Este insight es una observación general y
                          no tiene una dimensión específica asociada.
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            <section className="panel cycle-learning-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>LEARNING CANDIDATE</span>
                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  {learningCandidate ? "REGISTERED" : "DRAFT"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {!selectedInsight || !comparisonLeftCycle || !comparisonRightCycle ? (
                <div className="cycle-learning-empty">
                  Select an insight from the comparison to prepare a learning candidate.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="cycle-learning-content">
                  <div className="cycle-learning-source">
                    <span>SOURCE INSIGHT</span>
                    <strong>{selectedInsight.title}</strong>
                    <small>
                      {getCycleRequestText(comparisonLeftCycle)} ↔ {getCycleRequestText(comparisonRightCycle)}
                    </small>
                  </div>

                  {learningCandidate?.insightId === selectedInsight.id ? (
                    <>
                      <div className="cycle-learning-status">
                        <span>LEARNING STATUS</span>
                        <strong>{learningCandidate.status === "promoted" ? "PROMOTED TO SEMANTIC MEMORY" : "STAGED FOR LEARNING"}</strong>
                      </div>

                      <div className="cycle-learning-knowledge">
                        <span>REUSABLE KNOWLEDGE</span>
                        <p>{learningCandidate.knowledge}</p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="cycle-learning-evidence">
                        <span>EVIDENCE</span>
                        <strong>{learningCandidate.evidenceKeys.length} DIMENSIONS · {learningCandidate.status === "promoted" ? "SEMANTIC MEMORY" : "LOCAL STORE"}</strong>
                      </div>

                      {learningCandidate.status === "promoted" ? (
                        <div className="cycle-learning-promoted">
                          <strong>LEARNING PROMOTED</strong>
                          <span>Stored in NEXUS semantic memory.</span>
                          {learningCandidate.memoryKey ? (
                            <small>{learningCandidate.memoryKey}</small>
                          ) : null}
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="cycle-learning-button"
                          onClick={promoteLearningCandidate}
                        >
                          PROMOTE TO SEMANTIC MEMORY
                        </button>
                      )}

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="cycle-learning-button secondary"
                        onClick={clearLearningCandidate}
                      >
                        CLEAR CANDIDATE
                      </button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="cycle-learning-note">
                        Registering this candidate stores it in the local Learning Candidate Store. It does not write episodic memory or alter NEXUS reasoning.
                      </div>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="cycle-learning-button"
                        onClick={registerLearningCandidate}
                      >
                        REGISTER LEARNING CANDIDATE
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            <section className="panel cycle-learning-store-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>LEARNING CANDIDATE STORE</span>
                <span className="panel-indicator">
                  {learningCandidates.length} STORED
                </span>
              </div>

              {learningCandidates.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="cycle-learning-empty">
                  No learning candidates have been staged yet.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="cycle-learning-store-list">
                  {learningCandidates.map((candidate) => (
                    <button
                      key={`${candidate.insightId}-${candidate.createdAt}-${candidate.sourceCycleIds.join("-")}`}
                      type="button"
                      className={`cycle-learning-store-item ${
                        learningCandidate === candidate
                          ? "cycle-learning-store-item-selected"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                      onClick={() => setLearningCandidate(candidate)}
                    >
                      <span className="cycle-learning-store-marker">✦</span>
                      <span className="cycle-learning-store-summary">
                        <strong>{candidate.title}</strong>
                        <small>
                          {candidate.sourceCycleIds.length} CYCLES · {candidate.evidenceKeys.length} EVIDENCE · {candidate.status === "promoted" ? "PROMOTED" : "STAGED"} · {new Date(candidate.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString()}
                        </small>
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="cycle-learning-store-note">
                STAGED KNOWLEDGE · PROMOTION TO SEMANTIC MEMORY REMAINS EXPLICIT
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="panel result-panel">
              <div className="panel-header">
                <span>RESULT</span>
              </div>

              <div className="result-content">
                <div className="result-icon">
                  {presence.state === "failed"
                    ? "!"
                    : "✓"}
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="result-title">
                    {resultTitle}
                  </div>

                  <p>
                    {presence.execution?.result
                      ? "Execution completed."
                      : executionStatus ===
                          "running"
                        ? "NEXUS is executing the requested action."
                        : verificationMessage}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="verification-badge">
                <span>
                  {verificationStatus ===
                  "verified"
                    ? "✓"
                    : "•"}
                </span>

                {verificationStatus
                  .replaceAll("_", " ")
                  .toUpperCase()}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>

          <form
            className="command-bar"
            onSubmit={executeCommand}
          >
            <div className="command-prefix">
              NEXUS
            </div>

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder={
                isSubmitting
                  ? "NEXUS está ejecutando..."
                  : "Escribí una solicitud..."
              }
              aria-label="NEXUS request"
              value={command}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setCommand(event.target.value)
              }
              disabled={isSubmitting}
            />

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="command-button"
              aria-label="Send request"
              disabled={
                isSubmitting ||
                command.trim().length === 0
              }
            >
              {isSubmitting ? "…" : "↑"}
            </button>
          </form>

          {commandError && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="command-error"
            >
              {commandError}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

export default App;


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/App.tsx ---



===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/Assistant.tsx
===========================================================================


import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "./assistant.css";

type DesktopBridge = {
  setExpanded: (expanded: boolean) => void;
};

declare global {
  interface Window {
    nexusDesktop?: DesktopBridge;
  }
}

export default function Assistant() {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [command, setCommand] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState("");
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.classList.add("assistant-window");

    return () => {
      document.body.classList.remove("assistant-window");
    };
  }, []);

  function togglePanel() {
    const next = !expanded;
    setExpanded(next);
    setFeedback("");
    window.nexusDesktop?.setExpanded(next);

    if (next) {
      window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 120);
    }
  }

  async function submitCommand(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const text = command.trim();
    if (!text || busy) return;

    setBusy(true);
    setFeedback("NEXUS está procesando la instrucción…");

    try {
      const response = await fetch("http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/execute", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Accept: "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ text }),
      });

      const payload: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);

      if (!response.ok) {
        const errorMessage =
          typeof payload === "object" &&
          payload !== null &&
          "error" in payload &&
          typeof payload.error === "string"
            ? payload.error
            : `Error HTTP ${response.status}`;

        throw new Error(errorMessage);
      }

      if (typeof payload === "string") {
        setFeedback(payload);
      } else if (payload && typeof payload === "object") {
        const data = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
        const message = ["message", "response", "result", "output", "text"]
          .map((key) => data[key])
          .find((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0);

        setFeedback(
          typeof message === "string"
            ? message
            : JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2),
        );
      } else {
        setFeedback("Instrucción enviada. No se recibió contenido de respuesta.");
      }

      setCommand("");
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "No se pudo enviar la instrucción.",
      );
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className={`presence ${expanded ? "presence-expanded" : ""}`}>
      {!expanded ? (
        <button
          className="presence-orb"
          type="button"
          aria-label="Activar NEXUS"
          title="Activar NEXUS"
          onClick={togglePanel}
        >
          <span className="orb-halo" />
          <span className="orb-core" />
        </button>
      ) : (
        <section className="assistant-panel">
          <header className="assistant-header">
            <span className="assistant-brand">
              <span className="mini-orb" />
              NEXUS
              <small>PRESENCE</small>
            </span>
            <button
              className="assistant-close"
              type="button"
              onClick={togglePanel}
              aria-label="Volver al modo discreto"
              title="Minimizar"
            >
              −
            </button>
          </header>

          <form className="assistant-form" onSubmit={submitCommand}>
            <input
              ref={inputRef}
              value={command}
              onChange={(event) => setCommand(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Escribí una instrucción…"
              aria-label="Instrucción para NEXUS"
              disabled={busy}
            />
            <button
              className="assistant-send"
              type="submit"
              disabled={busy || !command.trim()}
              aria-label="Enviar instrucción"
              title="Enviar"
            >
              ↑
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="assistant-feedback" aria-live="polite">
            {busy && <span className="feedback-dot" />}
            <pre>{feedback || "Disponible para recibir instrucciones."}</pre>
          </div>

          <footer className="assistant-footer">
            <span>{busy ? "PROCESANDO" : "RUNTIME LOCAL"}</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="assistant-mic"
              onClick={() =>
                setFeedback(
                  "La entrada por voz se integrará en la siguiente etapa.",
                )
              }
              title="Entrada por voz: próxima etapa"
              aria-label="Entrada por voz"
            >
              MIC
            </button>
          </footer>
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/presence/adapter.ts
===========================================================================


import { mockPresence } from "./mockPresence";
import type {
  NexusPresenceEvent,
  NexusPresenceState,
  NexusPresenceStateModel,
} from "./types";

export interface PresenceAdapter {
  getCurrentState(): NexusPresenceStateModel;

  subscribe(
    listener: (state: NexusPresenceStateModel) => void,
  ): () => void;
}

const PRESENCE_API_URL =
  "http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/presence";

const PRESENCE_EVENTS_URL =
  "http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/presence/events";

const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 1000;

class HttpPresenceAdapter implements PresenceAdapter {
  private state: NexusPresenceStateModel = mockPresence;

  private listeners = new Set<
    (state: NexusPresenceStateModel) => void
  >();

  private pollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null =
    null;

  private eventSource: EventSource | null = null;

  private refreshing = false;

  private sseConnected = false;

  /*
   * Timestamp of the first presence event of the current
   * cognitive cycle.
   */
  private currentCycleStartedAt: string | null = null;

  /*
   * Presence events belonging exclusively to the current
   * cognitive cycle.
   */
  private currentCycleEvents: NexusPresenceEvent[] = [];

  getCurrentState(): NexusPresenceStateModel {
    return this.state;
  }

  subscribe(
    listener: (state: NexusPresenceStateModel) => void,
  ): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);

    listener(this.state);

    void this.refresh();

    this.startSSE();

    this.startPolling();

    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);

      if (this.listeners.size === 0) {
        this.stopSSE();
        this.stopPolling();
      }
    };
  }

  private startSSE(): void {
    if (this.eventSource !== null) {
      return;
    }

    if (typeof EventSource === "undefined") {
      return;
    }

    try {
      const eventSource = new EventSource(
        PRESENCE_EVENTS_URL,
      );

      this.eventSource = eventSource;

      eventSource.onopen = () => {
        this.sseConnected = true;
      };

      eventSource.addEventListener(
        "presence",
        (event) => {
          this.handlePresenceEvent(event);
        },
      );

      eventSource.onerror = () => {
        this.sseConnected = false;

        /*
         * EventSource automatically attempts to reconnect.
         *
         * HTTP snapshot polling remains available as fallback.
         */
      };
    } catch {
      this.sseConnected = false;
      this.eventSource = null;
    }
  }

  private stopSSE(): void {
    if (this.eventSource === null) {
      return;
    }

    this.eventSource.close();

    this.eventSource = null;
    this.sseConnected = false;
  }

  private handlePresenceEvent(
    event: MessageEvent<string>,
  ): void {
    try {
      const payload = JSON.parse(
        event.data,
      ) as {
        state?: string;
        timestamp?: string;
        message?: string | null;
        data?: unknown;
      };

      if (!payload.state) {
        return;
      }

      const state = this.normalizeState(
        payload.state,
      );

      if (state === null) {
        return;
      }

      const timestamp =
        payload.timestamp ??
        new Date().toISOString();

      /*
       * LISTENING marks the beginning of a new cognitive
       * cycle.
       *
       * Everything that was previously visible in the
       * timeline belongs to an older cycle.
       */
      if (state === "listening") {
        this.startNewCycle(timestamp);
      }

      const presenceEvent: NexusPresenceEvent = {
        id: `sse-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
          .toString(36)
          .slice(2, 8)}`,
        type: state,
        timestamp,
        message:
          payload.message ??
          undefined,
        data: payload.data,
      };

      this.addCurrentCycleEvent(
        presenceEvent,
      );

      this.state = {
        ...this.state,
        state,
        events: this.currentCycleEvents,
      };

      this.emit();

      /*
       * Terminal states trigger a complete snapshot refresh.
       *
       * The snapshot supplies objective, plan, execution,
       * verification, memory and learning details while the
       * current-cycle SSE events remain the timeline source.
       */
      if (
        state === "completed" ||
        state === "failed"
      ) {
        void this.refresh();
      }
    } catch {
      /*
       * Invalid SSE payloads must never break the adapter.
       */
    }
  }

  private startNewCycle(
    timestamp: string,
  ): void {
    this.currentCycleStartedAt = timestamp;
    this.currentCycleEvents = [];
  }

  private addCurrentCycleEvent(
    event: NexusPresenceEvent,
  ): void {
    const exists =
      this.currentCycleEvents.some(
        (existing) =>
          existing.timestamp ===
            event.timestamp &&
          existing.type === event.type &&
          existing.message ===
            event.message,
      );

    if (exists) {
      return;
    }

    this.currentCycleEvents = [
      ...this.currentCycleEvents,
      event,
    ];
  }

  private normalizeState(
    value: string,
  ): NexusPresenceState | null {
    const states: NexusPresenceState[] = [
      "idle",
      "listening",
      "understanding",
      "thinking",
      "deciding",
      "executing",
      "verifying",
      "completed",
      "needs_confirmation",
      "failed",
      "recovering",
    ];

    if (
      states.includes(
        value as NexusPresenceState,
      )
    ) {
      return value as NexusPresenceState;
    }

    return null;
  }

  private startPolling(): void {
    if (this.pollTimer !== null) {
      return;
    }

    this.pollTimer = setInterval(() => {
      void this.refresh();
    }, POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  private stopPolling(): void {
    if (this.pollTimer === null) {
      return;
    }

    clearInterval(this.pollTimer);

    this.pollTimer = null;
  }

  private async refresh(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.refreshing) {
      return;
    }

    this.refreshing = true;

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        PRESENCE_API_URL,
        {
          method: "GET",
          headers: {
            Accept: "application/json",
          },
        },
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          `Presence API returned HTTP ${response.status}`,
        );
      }

      const nextState =
        (await response.json()) as NexusPresenceStateModel;

      if (
        !this.shouldAcceptSnapshot(
          nextState,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      /*
       * Preserve the SSE event timeline whenever a current
       * cycle exists.
       *
       * The snapshot is still responsible for all the richer
       * cognitive data.
       */
      const events =
        this.resolveSnapshotEvents(
          nextState,
        );

      this.state = {
        ...nextState,
        events,
      };

      this.emit();
    } catch {
      /*
       * Preserve the last known state.
       */
    } finally {
      this.refreshing = false;
    }
  }

  private resolveSnapshotEvents(
    nextState: NexusPresenceStateModel,
  ): NexusPresenceEvent[] {
    /*
     * Once an SSE cycle has started, Presence events are the
     * authoritative source for the live/current timeline.
     */
    if (
      this.currentCycleStartedAt !== null
    ) {
      return this.currentCycleEvents;
    }

    /*
     * Before the first live cycle exists, use the snapshot.
     *
     * The snapshot may contain multiple historical cognitive
     * cycles. Keep only the latest one so the console timeline
     * represents the current cognitive cycle rather than
     * historical runtime activity.
     */
    return this.filterToLatestSnapshotCycle(
      nextState,
    );
  }

  private filterToLatestSnapshotCycle(
  presence: NexusPresenceStateModel,
): NexusPresenceEvent[] {
  if (presence.events.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  /*
   * Preferred boundary:
   *
   * The runtime stores the user request timestamp.
   * When available, it represents the beginning of the
   * latest request/cognitive cycle.
   */
  const requestTimestamp =
    presence.request?.timestamp;

  if (requestTimestamp) {
    const requestTime =
      Date.parse(requestTimestamp);

    if (!Number.isNaN(requestTime)) {
      return presence.events.filter(
        (event) => {
          const eventTime =
            Date.parse(event.timestamp);

          if (Number.isNaN(eventTime)) {
            return true;
          }

          return eventTime >= requestTime;
        },
      );
    }
  }

  /*
   * Snapshot fallback:
   *
   * Historical runtime snapshots use semantic event types
   * such as:
   *
   * request → context → intent → decision → objective
   * → plan → execution → verification → memory → learning
   *
   * Therefore REQUEST is the cycle boundary for snapshot
   * events.
   *
   * Find the LAST request event so only the newest cognitive
   * cycle remains visible in the console.
   */
  let latestRequestIndex = -1;

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index < presence.events.length;
    index += 1
  ) {
    const event = presence.events[index];

    if (event.type === "request") {
      latestRequestIndex = index;
    }
  }

  if (latestRequestIndex >= 0) {
    return presence.events.slice(
      latestRequestIndex,
    );
  }

  /*
   * If the snapshot contains no recognizable request
   * boundary, preserve the snapshot rather than guessing.
   */
  return presence.events;
}

  private shouldAcceptSnapshot(
    nextState: NexusPresenceStateModel,
  ): boolean {
    if (!this.sseConnected) {
      return true;
    }

    /*
     * Terminal snapshots are always accepted because they
     * contain the complete final execution information.
     */
    if (
      nextState.state === "completed" ||
      nextState.state === "failed"
    ) {
      return true;
    }

    return this.isSnapshotNewer(
      nextState,
      this.state,
    );
  }

  private isSnapshotNewer(
    nextState: NexusPresenceStateModel,
    currentState: NexusPresenceStateModel,
  ): boolean {
    const nextTimestamp =
      this.getLatestTimestamp(nextState);

    const currentTimestamp =
      this.getLatestTimestamp(currentState);

    if (
      nextTimestamp === null ||
      currentTimestamp === null
    ) {
      return true;
    }

    return (
      nextTimestamp >= currentTimestamp
    );
  }

  private getLatestTimestamp(
    state: NexusPresenceStateModel,
  ): number | null {
    const timestamps: number[] = [];

    if (state.request?.timestamp) {
      const value = Date.parse(
        state.request.timestamp,
      );

      if (!Number.isNaN(value)) {
        timestamps.push(value);
      }
    }

    for (const event of state.events) {
      const value = Date.parse(
        event.timestamp,
      );

      if (!Number.isNaN(value)) {
        timestamps.push(value);
      }
    }

    if (timestamps.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return Math.max(...timestamps);
  }

  private emit(): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      listener(this.state);
    }
  }
}

export const presenceAdapter =
  new HttpPresenceAdapter();

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/presence/cycles.ts
===========================================================================


import type {
  NexusPresenceStateModel,
} from "./types";

export type CognitiveCycleStatus =
  | "active"
  | "completed"
  | "failed";

export type CognitiveCycleEvent = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  timestamp: string;
  message?: string;
  data?: unknown;
};

export type CognitiveCycle = {
  id: string;
  startedAt: string;
  endedAt?: string;
  request?: CognitiveCycleEvent;
  events: CognitiveCycleEvent[];
  status: CognitiveCycleStatus;
  eventCount: number;
};

function getCycleStatus(
  events: CognitiveCycleEvent[],
): CognitiveCycleStatus {
  const lastEvent = events[events.length - 1];

  if (!lastEvent) {
    return "active";
  }

  if (
    lastEvent.type === "verification_failure" ||
    lastEvent.type === "failed"
  ) {
    return "failed";
  }

  if (
    lastEvent.type === "episode" ||
    lastEvent.type === "completed"
  ) {
    return "completed";
  }

  return "active";
}

function createCycle(
  events: CognitiveCycleEvent[],
): CognitiveCycle | null {
  if (events.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const request = events.find(
    (event) => event.type === "user_request",
  );

  const startedAt =
    request?.timestamp ??
    events[0].timestamp;

  const lastEvent =
    events[events.length - 1];

  const status = getCycleStatus(events);

  return {
    id: request?.id ?? events[0].id,
    startedAt,
    endedAt:
      status === "active"
        ? undefined
        : lastEvent.timestamp,
    request,
    events,
    status,
    eventCount: events.length,
  };
}

export function buildCycleHistory(
  presence: NexusPresenceStateModel,
): CognitiveCycle[] {
  const sourceEvents = presence.events;

  if (sourceEvents.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }

  const cycles: CognitiveCycle[] = [];
  let currentEvents: CognitiveCycleEvent[] = [];

  for (const event of sourceEvents) {
    const normalizedEvent: CognitiveCycleEvent = {
      id: event.id,
      type: event.type,
      timestamp: event.timestamp,
      message: event.message,
      data: event.data,
    };

    if (
      normalizedEvent.type === "user_request" &&
      currentEvents.length > 0
    ) {
      const previousCycle = createCycle(
        currentEvents,
      );

      if (previousCycle) {
        cycles.push(previousCycle);
      }

      currentEvents = [];
    }

    currentEvents.push(normalizedEvent);
  }

  const lastCycle = createCycle(
    currentEvents,
  );

  if (lastCycle) {
    cycles.push(lastCycle);
  }

  return cycles;
}

export function getCurrentCycle(
  presence: NexusPresenceStateModel,
): CognitiveCycle | null {
  const cycles = buildCycleHistory(presence);

  return cycles.length > 0
    ? cycles[cycles.length - 1]
    : null;
}

export function getHistoricalCycles(
  presence: NexusPresenceStateModel,
): CognitiveCycle[] {
  const cycles = buildCycleHistory(presence);

  if (cycles.length <= 1) {
    return [];
  }

  return cycles.slice(0, -1);
}

ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/presence/learningCandidates.ts

export type StoredLearningCandidate = {
  id: string;
  insightId: string;
  title: string;
  knowledge: string;
  sourceCycleIds: string[];
  evidenceKeys: string[];
  createdAt: string;
  status?: "staged" | "promoted";
  promotedAt?: string;
  memoryKey?: string;
};

const STORAGE_KEY = "nexus.learning-candidates.v1";

function isCandidate(value: unknown): value is StoredLearningCandidate {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
    return false;
  }

  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;

  return (
    (typeof candidate.id === "string" || candidate.id === undefined) &&
    typeof candidate.insightId === "string" &&
    typeof candidate.title === "string" &&
    typeof candidate.knowledge === "string" &&
    Array.isArray(candidate.sourceCycleIds) &&
    candidate.sourceCycleIds.every((item) => typeof item === "string") &&
    Array.isArray(candidate.evidenceKeys) &&
    candidate.evidenceKeys.every((item) => typeof item === "string") &&
    typeof candidate.createdAt === "string"
  );
}

export function loadLearningCandidates(): StoredLearningCandidate[] {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

    if (!raw) {
      return [];
    }

    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);

    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      return [];
    }

    return parsed.filter(isCandidate).map((candidate) => ({
      ...candidate,
      id:
        typeof candidate.id === "string"
          ? candidate.id
          : `insight-${candidate.insightId}-${candidate.sourceCycleIds.slice().sort().join("--")}`,
      status: candidate.status ?? "staged",
    }));
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function persist(candidates: StoredLearningCandidate[]): void {
  try {
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(candidates),
    );
  } catch {
    // Local storage is an optional UI persistence layer.
  }
}

export function saveLearningCandidate(
  candidate: StoredLearningCandidate,
): void {
  const current = loadLearningCandidates();

  const exists = current.some(
    (item) =>
      item.insightId === candidate.insightId &&
      item.sourceCycleIds.length === candidate.sourceCycleIds.length &&
      item.sourceCycleIds.every((id) => candidate.sourceCycleIds.includes(id)),
  );

  if (exists) {
    return;
  }

  persist([candidate, ...current]);
}

export function updateLearningCandidate(
  candidate: StoredLearningCandidate,
): void {
  const current = loadLearningCandidates();
  const index = current.findIndex((item) => item.id === candidate.id);

  if (index === -1) {
    return;
  }

  current[index] = candidate;
  persist(current);
}

export function removeLearningCandidate(
  candidate: StoredLearningCandidate,
): void {
  const current = loadLearningCandidates();

  persist(
    current.filter(
      (item) =>
        !(
          item.insightId === candidate.insightId &&
          item.createdAt === candidate.createdAt &&
          item.sourceCycleIds.length === candidate.sourceCycleIds.length &&
          item.sourceCycleIds.every((id) => candidate.sourceCycleIds.includes(id))
        ),
    ),
  );
}

ARCHIVO: apps/console/src/presence/mockPresence.ts

import type { NexusPresenceStateModel } from "./types";

export const mockPresence: NexusPresenceStateModel = {
  state: "completed",

  request: {
    text: "Cerrá Chrome",

export type NexusPresenceState =
  | "idle"
  | "listening"
  | "understanding"
  | "thinking"
  | "deciding"
  | "executing"
  | "verifying"
  | "completed"
  | "needs_confirmation"
  | "failed"
  | "recovering";

export type NexusDecisionType =
  | "execute"
  | "confirm"
  | "reject"
  | "recover"
  | "none";

export type NexusExecutionStatus =
  | "pending"
  | "running"
  | "completed"
  | "failed";

export type NexusVerificationStatus =
  | "pending"
  | "verified"
  | "verified_with_limited_evidence"
  | "failed";

export type NexusMemoryStatus =
  | "pending"
  | "not_required"
  | "stored"
  | "failed";

export type NexusLearningStatus =
  | "pending"
  | "not_required"
  | "learned"
  | "failed";

export type NexusPlanStepStatus =
  | "pending"
  | "running"
  | "completed"
  | "failed"
  | "skipped";

export interface NexusPresenceRequest {
  text: string;
  timestamp?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresenceContext {
  recent_interactions?: unknown[];
  recent_episodes?: unknown[];
  semantic_knowledge?: unknown[];
  last_request?: unknown;
  last_intent?: unknown;
  last_result?: unknown;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

export interface NexusPresenceIntent {
  name: string;
  confidence: number;
}

export interface NexusPresenceDecision {
  type: NexusDecisionType;
  reason?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresenceObjective {
  description: string;
}

export interface NexusPresencePlanStep {
  id?: string;
  action: string;
  status: NexusPlanStepStatus;
  description?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresencePlan {
  steps: NexusPresencePlanStep[];
}

export interface NexusPresenceExecution {
  status: NexusExecutionStatus;
  action?: string;
  result?: unknown;
  error?: string | null;
}

export interface NexusPresenceVerification {
  status: NexusVerificationStatus;
  confidence?: number;
  message?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresenceMemory {
  status: NexusMemoryStatus;
  entries?: number;
  message?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresenceLearning {
  status: NexusLearningStatus;
  message?: string;
}

export interface NexusPresenceEvent {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  timestamp: string;
  message?: string;
  data?: unknown;
}

export interface NexusPresenceStateModel {
  state: NexusPresenceState;

  request: NexusPresenceRequest | null;

  context: NexusPresenceContext | null;

  intent: NexusPresenceIntent | null;

  decision: NexusPresenceDecision | null;

  objective: NexusPresenceObjective | null;

  plan: NexusPresencePlan | null;

  execution: NexusPresenceExecution | null;

  verification: NexusPresenceVerification | null;

  memory: NexusPresenceMemory | null;

  learning: NexusPresenceLearning | null;

  events: NexusPresenceEvent[];
}

# =====================================================
# DB WAIT
# =====================================================

def wait_for_db():
    import time

    for _ in range(10):
        try:
            conn = get_connection()
            conn.cursor().execute("SELECT 1")
            conn.close()
            print("✅ DB lista")
            return
        except Exception:
            print("⏳ Esperando DB...")
            time.sleep(2)

    raise Exception("DB no disponible")


# =====================================================
# LIFESPAN
# =====================================================

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):

    logging.info("🚀 Nexus iniciando...")

    wait_for_db()
    init_db()

    manager_module.manager = JobManager()
    await manager_module.manager.start()

    Scheduler()

    load_plugins()

    asyncio.create_task(autonomous_loop())

    start_folder_trigger()

    for m in pull_memory():
        save_memory(m)

    workflows = load_workflows()

    for w in workflows:
        trigger = w.get("trigger")
        if trigger and trigger.get("type") == "filesystem":
            from events.watcher import watcher
            watcher.watch(trigger.get("path"), w.get("name"))

    add_job(600, [
        {"action": "create_folder", "value": "PDF"}
    ])

    watch(".", ".pdf", [
        {"action": "create_folder", "value": "PDF"}
    ])

    yield

    logging.info("🛑 Nexus detenido")

# =====================================================
# 🔐 AUTH MIDDLEWARE
# =====================================================

@app.middleware("http")
async def auth(request: Request, call_next):

    if request.method == "OPTIONS":
        return await call_next(request)

    path = request.url.path

    PUBLIC_PATHS = {
        "/",
        "/health",
        "/metrics",
        "/docs",
        "/openapi.json",
        "/auth/login",
        "/auth/register",
    }

    if path in PUBLIC_PATHS or path.startswith("/auth") or path.startswith("/ws"):
        return await call_next(request)

    auth_header = request.headers.get("Authorization")

    if not auth_header:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"error": "Missing token"})

    try:
        parts = auth_header.split(" ")

        if len(parts) != 2 or parts[0] != "Bearer":
            raise Exception("Invalid format")

        token = parts[1]

        user = decode_token(token)

        if not user:
            raise Exception("Invalid token")

        request.state.user = user

    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ AUTH ERROR:", str(e))
        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"error": "Invalid token"})

    return await call_next(request)

@app.post("/nexus/mode")
async def set_mode(data: dict):

    requested = data.get("mode")

    approved, reason = evaluate_mode_request(requested)

    nexus_state.apply_mode(approved)

    await ws.broadcast({
        "type": "mode.changed",
        "mode": approved
    })

    return {
        "requested": requested,
        "applied": approved
    }

ARCHIVO: backend/actions/planner.py

import json
import ollama
from actions.system_actions import execute_action


SYSTEM = """
Sos Nexus Planner.

Convertí la orden del usuario en un PLAN JSON.

Formato:
{
  "steps": [
    {"action": "create_folder", "value": "PDF"},
    {"action": "move_files", "value": {"ext": ".pdf", "to": "PDF"}}
  ]
}

Acciones disponibles:
- open_app
- open_file
- create_folder
- move_files
- run_cmd

Solo devolver JSON.
"""


def run_plan(user_message: str):

    res = ollama.chat(
        model="llama3",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM},
            {"role": "user", "content": user_message}
        ]
    )

    content = res["message"]["content"]

    try:
        plan = json.loads(content)
    except:
        return "No pude generar un plan."

    outputs = []

    for step in plan.get("steps", []):
        out = execute_action(step["action"], step["value"])
        outputs.append(out)

    return "\n".join(outputs)



--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/actions/planner.py ---

ARCHIVO: backend/actions/registry.py

ACTIONS = {}


def register_action(name, fn):
    ACTIONS[name] = fn


def get_action(name):
    return ACTIONS.get(name)


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/actions/registry.py ---



===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/actions/system_actions.py
===========================================================================


import os
import subprocess
import shutil
import time
import webbrowser
from pathlib import Path
import asyncio

import psutil

from plugins.loader import run_plugin, load_plugins
from security.policy import requires_confirm, is_path_allowed
from security.logger import log
from learning.tracker import track

from integrations.http_client import http_request
from integrations.slack import send_slack
from integrations.email import send_email

from actions.registry import get_action


# ==================================================
# 🔥 CARGAR PLUGINS
# ==================================================

load_plugins()


# ==================================================
# 🔥 ALIASES
# ==================================================

ALIASES = {
    "open_browser": "open_app",
    "launch_app": "open_app",
    "start_app": "open_app",
    "delete": "delete_file",
    "remove_file": "delete_file",
    "copy": "copy_file",
    "move": "move_files",
}


def normalize(action: str):
    return ALIASES.get(action, action)


# ==================================================
# 🔐 ROLES
# ==================================================

def check_role(user, action):

    role = user.get("role")

    if role == "admin":
        return True

    if role == "staff" and action in [
        "delete_file", "run_cmd", "kill_process"
    ]:
        return False

    if role == "viewer":
        return False

    return True


# ==================================================
# 🔐 SEGURIDAD
# ==================================================

def confirm_if_needed(action, value, auto=True):

    if auto:
        return True

    try:
        resp = input(f"Confirmar acción {action}? (y/n): ")
        return resp.lower() == "y"
    except:
        return False


def sandbox_check(value):
    if isinstance(value, str) and ("\\" in value or "/" in value):
        if not is_path_allowed(value):
            return False
    return True


# ==================================================
# 🔥 MAIN EXECUTOR (ASYNC)
# ==================================================

async def execute_action(action, value, user=None):

    if user is None:
        user = {"role": "admin"}

    action = normalize(action)

    if not check_role(user, action):
        return "⛔ Permiso denegado"

    if not sandbox_check(value):
        return "⛔ Ruta no permitida por seguridad"

    if not confirm_if_needed(action, value):
        return "Acción cancelada"

    result = None

    plugin = get_action(action)

    if plugin:
      result = plugin(value)
      return result

    # ==================================================
    # 📦 APPS
    # ==================================================

    if action == "open_app":

        shortcuts = {
            "chrome": r"C:\Program Files\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe",
            "edge": r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe",
            "notepad": "notepad",
            "calc": "calc",
        }

        value = shortcuts.get(str(value).lower(), value)
        subprocess.Popen(value)
        result = f"Abriendo {value}"

    elif action == "open_url":

        webbrowser.open(value)
        result = f"Abriendo URL {value}"

    # ==================================================
    # 📁 FILESYSTEM
    # ==================================================

    elif action == "list_dir":
        files = os.listdir(value or ".")
        result = "\n".join(files)

    elif action == "read_file":
        result = Path(value).read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    elif action == "write_file":
        Path(value["path"]).write_text(value["content"], encoding="utf-8")
        result = f"Archivo guardado: {value['path']}"

    elif action == "delete_file":
        os.remove(value)
        result = f"Archivo eliminado: {value}"

    elif action == "copy_file":
        shutil.copy(value["src"], value["dst"])
        result = "Archivo copiado"

    elif action == "rename_file":
        os.rename(value["src"], value["dst"])
        result = "Archivo renombrado"

    elif action == "create_folder":
        Path(value).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        result = f"Carpeta creada: {value}"

    elif action == "move_files":

        ext = value["ext"]
        to = value["to"]

        Path(to).mkdir(exist_ok=True)

        moved = 0

        for f in os.listdir("."):
            if f.lower().endswith(ext):
                shutil.move(f, os.path.join(to, f))
                moved += 1

        result = f"{moved} archivos movidos a {to}"

    # ==================================================
    # 🌐 INTEGRACIONES EXTERNAS
    # ==================================================

    elif action == "http_request":

        result = await http_request(
            value["method"],
            value["url"],
            value.get("headers"),
            value.get("body"),
            value.get("timeout", 10)
        )

    elif action == "send_slack":

        result = await send_slack(
            value["webhook_url"],
            value["message"]
        )

    elif action == "send_email":

        result = await send_email(
            value["smtp_host"],
            value["smtp_port"],
            value["username"],
            value["password"],
            value["to"],
            value["subject"],
            value["body"]
        )

    # ==================================================
    # 🖥 SISTEMA
    # ==================================================

    elif action == "list_processes":
        names = [p.info['name'] for p in psutil.process_iter(['name'])]
        result = "\n".join(names)

    elif action == "kill_process":

        for p in psutil.process_iter(['name']):
            if p.info['name'].lower() == value.lower():
                p.kill()
                result = f"{value} finalizado"
                break
        else:
            result = "Proceso no encontrado"

    elif action == "get_time":
        from datetime import datetime
        result = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")

    elif action == "system_info":

        result = f"""
        OS: {os.name}
        CPU: {psutil.cpu_percent()}%
        RAM: {psutil.virtual_memory().percent}%
        """

    elif action == "sleep":
        await asyncio.sleep(float(value))
        result = f"Esperado {value}s"

    # ==================================================
    # 💻 SHELL
    # ==================================================

    elif action == "run_cmd":
        result = subprocess.getoutput(value)

    # ==================================================
    # 🔌 PLUGINS
    # ==================================================

    else:

        plugin_result = run_plugin(action, value)

        if plugin_result is not None:
            result = plugin_result
        else:
            result = f"Acción no reconocida: {action}"

    # ==================================================
    # 🔥 LOG + TRACK
    # ==================================================

    log(action, result)
    track(action, value)

    return result


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/actions/system_actions.py ---



===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/actions/plugins/__init__.py
===========================================================================


import importlib
import pkgutil


def load_plugins():

    for loader, name, is_pkg in pkgutil.iter_modules(__path__):
        importlib.import_module(f"{__name__}.{name}")

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/actions/plugins/filesystem.py
===========================================================================


import os
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

from actions.registry import register_action


def create_folder(value):

    Path(value).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    return f"Carpeta creada: {value}"


def list_dir(value):

    files = os.listdir(value or ".")

    return "\n".join(files)


def move_files(value):

    ext = value["ext"]
    to = value["to"]

    Path(to).mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    moved = 0

    for f in os.listdir("."):
        if f.lower().endswith(ext):
            shutil.move(f, os.path.join(to, f))
            moved += 1

    return f"{moved} archivos movidos a {to}"


register_action("create_folder", create_folder)
register_action("list_dir", list_dir)
register_action("move_files", move_files)


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/actions/plugins/filesystem.py ---

import requests

OLLAMA_URL = "http://localhost:11434/api/generate"


def ask_ollama(prompt):

    payload = {
        "model": "phi3",
        "prompt": prompt,
        "stream": False
    }

    r = requests.post(OLLAMA_URL, json=payload)

    data = r.json()

    return data["response"]

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/ai/planner.py
===========================================================================


import json
from ai.ollama_client import ask_ollama
from memory.nexus_memory import load_memory


PLANNER_PROMPT = """
You are an AI system planner.

Your task is to create a plan to automate a user request.

Return JSON only.

Schema:

{
 "goal": "user goal",
 "plan": [
   "step 1",
   "step 2"
 ]
}
"""


def create_plan(user_prompt):

    memory = load_memory()

    memory_context = json.dumps(memory)

    full_prompt = (
        PLANNER_PROMPT
        + "\n\nSystem memory:\n"
        + memory_context
        + "\n\nUser request:\n"
        + user_prompt
    )

    response = ask_ollama(full_prompt)

    try:
        plan = json.loads(response)
    except:
        raise Exception("Planner devolvió JSON inválido")

    return plan


def plan_to_workflow(plan):

    steps = []

    for p in plan["plan"]:

        text = p.lower()

        if "carpeta" in text:

            steps.append({
                "type": "action",
                "action": "create_folder",
                "value": "data"
            })

        elif "mover" in text:

            steps.append({
                "type": "action",
                "action": "move_files",
                "value": {
                    "ext": ".pdf",
                    "to": "PDF"
                }
            })

        elif "listar" in text:

            steps.append({
                "type": "action",
                "action": "list_dir",
                "value": "."
            })

    return {
        "name": "ai_generated_workflow",
        "trigger": "manual",
        "steps": steps
    }

===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/ai/workflow_generator.py
===========================================================================


import json
from ai.ollama_client import ask_ollama


SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are an automation engine.

Return ONLY valid JSON.

Schema:

{
 "name": "workflow_name",
 "trigger": "manual",
 "steps":[
   {
     "type":"action",
     "action":"create_folder",
     "value":"folder"
   }
 ]
}

Allowed actions:

create_folder
move_files
list_dir
read_file
write_file
delete_file
run_cmd
open_url
"""


def generate_workflow(prompt):

    full_prompt = SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n\nUser request:\n" + prompt

    response = ask_ollama(full_prompt)

    try:
        workflow = json.loads(response)
    except:
        raise Exception("LLM devolvió JSON inválido")

    return workflow


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/ai/workflow_generator.py ---



===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/api/ai.py
===========================================================================


from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from brain.ai_planner import generate_workflow
from workflows.validator import validate_workflow
from workflows.repository import save_workflow_version

router = APIRouter(prefix="/ai", tags=["AI"])


@router.post("/workflow")
async def ai_workflow(data: dict):

    task = data.get("task")

    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="task requerido"
        )

    try:

        # 1️⃣ generar workflow con IA
        wf = generate_workflow(task)

        # 2️⃣ validar estructura
        wf = validate_workflow(wf)

        # 3️⃣ guardar en DB
        save_workflow_version(
            wf["name"],
            wf.get("trigger", "manual"),
            wf["steps"]
        )

        return {
            "ok": True,
            "workflow": wf
        }

    except Exception as e:

        return {
            "ok": False,
            "error": str(e)
        }


--- FIN DEL ARCHIVO: backend/api/ai.py ---



===========================================================================
ARCHIVO: backend/api/ai_builder.py
===========================================================================


import json
import re
from core.llm import generate_steps
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from auth.deps import get_current_user

router = APIRouter(prefix="/ai-builder")


@router.post("/generate")
async def generate(data: dict, user=Depends(get_current_user)):

    prompt = data.get("prompt")

    raw = generate_steps(prompt)

    print("🧠 RAW LLM:", raw)

    try:
        # =========================
        # 🔥 EXTRAER SOLO JSON
        # =========================
        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", raw, re.DOTALL)

        if not match:
            raise Exception("No se encontró JSON")

        clean_json = match.group(0)

        parsed = json.loads(clean_json)

        steps = validate_steps(parsed.get("steps", []))

        return {"steps": steps}

    except Exception as e:

        print("❌ AI BUILDER ERROR:", str(e))

        return {
            "error": "LLM devolvió JSON inválido",
            "raw": raw
        }



# =========================
# FIX JSON (🔥 CLAVE)
# =========================

def fix_json(text: str):

    # cerrar llaves faltantes
    open_braces = text.count("{")
    close_braces = text.count("}")

    if open_braces > close_braces:
        text += "}" * (open_braces - close_braces)

    # cerrar corchetes faltantes
    open_brackets = text.count("[")
    close_brackets = text.count("]")

    if open_brackets > close_brackets:
        text += "]" * (open_brackets - close_brackets)

    return text


# =========================
# EXTRACT JSON
# =========================

def extract_json(text):

    match = re.search(r"\{.*", text, re.DOTALL)

    if match:
        return match.group(0)

    return text


# =========================
# VALIDATE
# =========================

VALID_ACTIONS = {
    "create_folder",
    "move_files",
    "open_url"
}


def validate_steps(steps):

    safe = []

    for s in steps:

        if s.get("action") not in VALID_ACTIONS:
            continue

        safe.append(s)

    return safe

from fastapi import APIRouter

import jobs.manager as manager_module

router = APIRouter(prefix="/approvals")


@router.post("/{job_id}")
async def approve(job_id: str):

    await manager_module.manager.resume_job(
        job_id
    )

    return {
        "success": True
    }
